Across five mathematics and physics benchmarks, self-organizing teams average 66.7% accuracy, versus 48.8% for their strongest member, 58.7% for compute-matched inference by that agent, and 59.0% for a perfect router over members' independent answers; on AIME 2026, they exceed this router by 13.4 points. Because gains vary across benchmarks, we ask when self-organizing collaboration helps. Across eight benchmarks, demonstrability (the organizational-psychology construct of whether a team can distinguish correct from incorrect reasoning) strongly tracks improvement over the strongest member (Spearman $ρ=0.90$, $p=0.005$): teams benefit most when correct reasoning can be recognized once it appears. More broadly, these results suggest that organization itself can become an agent capability: agent teams can learn how to reason together and produce solutions their members could not reach independently.

</details>


### [27] [Trustworthy Agentic AI: Failure Modes, Mitigation Strategies, and a Lifecycle Framework for Autonomous LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22712)
*Fayeq Jeelani Syed,Rehan Ahmad,Ali Al Bataineh,Aakriti Adhikari*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 系统梳理LLM agent的失效模式（间接提示注入、记忆污染、目标误泛化）与缓解策略，并提出可信agent开发生命周期框架，对agent可靠性设计有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: 本文综述基于大语言模型的智能体AI在安全、对齐、透明、隐私和合规五个维度上的可信性，梳理关键失效模式与缓解方法，并提出六阶段的可信智能体开发生命周期（TADL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI具备多步规划、工具使用、记忆和协作能力，比静态语言模型更有用，但也带来间接提示注入、记忆污染、跨会话数据泄露及工具调用导致现实行动等新的安全与运营风险，因此需要系统评估其可信性。

Method: 文章从安全性与鲁棒性、对齐与人类监督、透明度与可审计性、隐私与数据治理、法规合规五个相互关联维度进行综述；将关键失效模式归纳为统一分类体系；分析指令层级、上下文隔离、spotlighting、基于过程的监督、受限工具使用、隐私保护记忆等缓解方法，并区分有无实证支持；在此基础上提出六阶段TADL框架。

Result: 文章梳理了间接提示注入、后门触发器、目标错误泛化、记忆污染和跨会话数据泄露等失效模式及对应缓解技术；提出TADL，覆盖规范、设计、训练、评估、部署和监控，并为各阶段给出可信活动、预期证据和基于风险的决策门；同时指出TADL尚未实证验证，且当前基准存在不足。

Conclusion: 尽管TADL尚未经过实证验证，但它为开发和评估更安全、更可问责的智能体系统提供了结构化基础；未来研究需弥补当前基准的差距，并优先推进相关方向。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型构建的智能体AI系统能够进行多步规划、使用外部工具、在记忆中保留信息，并与其他智能体协调。这些能力使它们比静态语言模型更有用，但也引入了新的安全与运营风险。来自网站、电子邮件、文档和数据库的不可信内容可能进入与系统指令相同的上下文；持久记忆可能跨会话携带被污染的信息；对外部工具的访问可能将不正确的模型响应转化为有后果的现实世界行动。本文围绕五个相互关联的维度综述智能体AI的可信性：安全性与鲁棒性、对齐与人类监督、透明度与可审计性、隐私与数据治理，以及法规合规。它将关键失效模式，包括间接提示注入、后门触发器、目标错误泛化、记忆污染和跨会话数据泄露，组织为一个统一的分类体系。它还考察主要缓解方法，如指令层级、上下文隔离、spotlighting、基于过程的监督、受限工具使用和隐私保护记忆，同时区分有经验证据支持的技术与仍主要停留在概念层面的技术。在此分析基础上，我们提出可信智能体开发生命周期（TADL），一个涵盖规范、设计、训练、评估、部署和监控的六阶段框架。针对每个阶段，TADL识别相关可信活动、预期证据和基于风险的决策门。尽管TADL尚未经过实证验证，但它为开发和评估更安全、更可问责的智能体系统提供了结构化基础。文章最后指出当前基准的不足，并概述未来研究的优先事项。

Original abstract: Agentic AI systems built on large language models can plan over multiple steps, use external tools, retain information in memory, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make them more useful than static language models, but they also introduce new security and operational risks. Untrusted content from websites, emails, documents, and databases can enter the same context as system instructions; persistent memory can carry compromised information across sessions; and access to external tools can turn an incorrect model response into a consequential real-world action. This article reviews the trustworthiness of agentic AI across five interconnected dimensions: safety and robustness, alignment and human oversight, transparency and auditability, privacy and data governance, and regulatory compliance. It organizes key failure modes, including indirect prompt injection, backdoor triggers, goal misgeneralization, memory contamination, and cross-session data leakage, into a unified taxonomy. It also examines major mitigation approaches, such as instruction hierarchies, context isolation, spotlighting, process-based supervision, constrained tool use, and privacy-preserving memory, while distinguishing techniques supported by empirical evidence from those that remain largely conceptual. Building on this analysis, we introduce the Trustworthy Agent Development Lifecycle (TADL), a six-phase framework covering specification, design, training, evaluation, deployment, and monitoring. For each phase, TADL identifies relevant trust activities, expected evidence, and risk-based decision gates. Although TADL has not yet been empirically validated, it provides a structured foundation for developing and evaluating more secure and accountable agentic systems. The article concludes by identifying gaps in current benchmarks and outlining priorities for future research.

</details>


### [28] [ProcessLight: Process Supervision for Large Language Model Based Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22746)
*Huaitao Zhao,Tianlong Zhou,Weijie Wang,Jiasheng Shi,Weixiong Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: This paper has been accepted by The 2026 Conference on Empirical Methods in Natural Language Processing (EMNLP 2026)

Selection reason: 提出可验证语义步骤分解与步级信用分配的RL过程监督方法，虽应用于交通信号控制，但步级过程奖励与step-level advantage机制可迁移到agent轨迹优化。

TL;DR: 针对现有LLM交通信号控制仅优化最终结果、无法区分推理步骤质量的问题，提出ProcessLight框架和STeP-PO强化学习方法，通过步骤级信用分配实现细粒度策略优化，在真实数据集上验证了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM TSC方法仅从最终结果优化，无法区分有效与有缺陷的推理步骤，导致有用或误导性步骤被联合更新，损害模型学习有效推理的能力。需要一种能分解决策过程并进行步骤级优化的方法。

Method: 提出ProcessLight框架，将信号决策分解为可验证的语义步骤；在此基础上开发STeP-PO（逐步交通过程策略优化），利用步骤质量分数评估局部推理质量，步骤重要性衡量各步骤对最终动作的影响，在语义步骤树结构上分配步骤级优势，并将优势传播至推理令牌，实现超越仅结果奖励的细粒度策略优化。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法在多个真实世界数据集上展现出优越性，代码已开源。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）最近因在人类可读推理生成方面的优势，被引入交通信号控制（TSC）作为决策智能体。然而，现有的LLM TSC方法仅从最终结果进行优化，无法区分有效与有缺陷的推理步骤，导致有用或误导性的步骤被联合更新，从而损害了模型对有效推理的学习。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了基于LLM的框架ProcessLight，将信号决策分解为可验证的语义步骤。在ProcessLight的基础上，我们进一步开发了逐步交通过程策略优化（STeP-PO），一种新颖的强化学习框架，通过步骤级信用分配来优化结构化推理过程。具体而言，STeP-PO使用步骤质量分数评估局部推理质量，并使用步骤重要性衡量每个步骤对最终动作的影响，然后在语义步骤树结构上分配步骤级优势。所得的步骤级优势被传播到推理令牌，从而实现超越仅结果奖励的细粒度策略优化。在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验证明了我们方法的优越性。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/wenzhaoabc/processlight 获取。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been introduced into traffic signal control (TSC) as decision agents due to their strengths in human-readable reasoning generation. Yet, existing LLM TSC methods optimize only from final outcomes and fail to distinguish valid from flawed reasoning steps, causing useful or misleading steps to be jointly updated and thus impairing the model's learning of effective reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose an LLM-based framework ProcessLight to decompose signal decisions into verifiable semantic steps. Building on ProcessLight, we further develop Step-wise Traffic Process Policy Optimization (STeP-PO), a novel reinforcement learning framework that optimizes structured reasoning processes through step-level credit assignment. Specifically, STeP-PO uses step quality scores to evaluate local reasoning quality and step importance to measure each step's influence on the final action, and then assigns step-level advantages over a semantic step tree structure. The resulting step-level advantages are propagated to reasoning tokens, enabling fine-grained policy optimization beyond outcome-only rewards. Extensive experiments over multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of our methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/wenzhaoabc/processlight.

</details>


### [29] [ISA-Bench: A Benchmark for Computational Reasoning Across Instruction Set Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22878)
*Aditya Pola,Arkaprava Majumdar,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: ISA-Bench以受限指令集编程游戏评测LLM代码生成，自带解析器/VM/验证器与结构化反馈，并给出推理-执行差距分析，对coding agent的反馈式迭代与benchmark设计有参考。

TL;DR: 提出ISA-Bench基准，用受限指令集编程游戏评估大语言模型的代码生成与推理，并发现推理—执行差距（REG）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准主要针对Python和Java等训练数据丰富的语言，难以评估模型在不熟悉计算模型中的推理能力，例如从单条减法指令推导算术、协调通信节点间的并行程序或将逻辑门连接成电路。

Method: 构建ISA-Bench，包含一组具有受限指令集的编程游戏；每个游戏提供由解析器、虚拟机和验证器组成的完整执行栈，支持自动化评估和结构化反馈下的迭代改进。

Result: 推理模型的平均求解率高于代码专用模型和通用模型，但不熟悉的语法仍是主要失败来源；迭代反馈能提升求解表现，但不同架构的增益差异显著。

Conclusion: 通过推理—执行差距（REG）分析，揭示模型在识别可行计算策略与在目标ISA中写出正确程序之间存在反复脱节；代码已开源。

中文摘要：大语言模型代码生成基准主要评估资源丰富的语言，如Python和Java，在这些语言中模型受益于大量训练数据。它们对于不熟悉的计算模型中的推理提供的证据有限：从单条减法指令推导算术运算、在通信节点间协调并行程序，或将逻辑门连接成电路。我们提出了ISA-Bench，一个具有受限指令集的编程游戏基准。对于每个游戏，我们提供完整的执行栈（解析器、虚拟机和验证器），从而能够进行自动化评估，并提供结构化反馈以支持迭代改进。推理模型比代码专用模型和通用模型获得更高的平均求解率，但不熟悉的语法仍然是失败的主要来源。模型通过迭代反馈能解决更多任务，不过不同架构之间的增益差异很大。我们引入了推理—执行差距（REG）分析，揭示了在识别可行的计算策略与将其表达为目标ISA中的正确程序之间反复出现的脱节。代码已开源。

Original abstract: Large language model code generation benchmarks primarily evaluate well-resourced languages like Python and Java, where models benefit from abundant training data. They provide limited evidence about reasoning in unfamiliar computational models: deriving arithmetic from a single subtract instruction, coordinating parallel programs across communicating nodes, or wiring logic gates into circuits. We present ISA-Bench, a benchmark of programming games with constrained instruction sets. For each game we provide a full execution stack (parser, VM, and verifier), enabling automated evaluation with structured feedback for iterative refinement. Reasoning models achieve higher average solve rates than code-specialized and general-purpose models, but unfamiliar syntax remains a major source of failure. Models solve more tasks with iterative feedback, though the gains vary substantially across architectures. We introduce a reasoning--execution gap (REG) analysis that reveals a recurring disconnect between identifying a plausible computational strategy and expressing it as a correct program in the target ISA. Code is open-sourced.

</details>


### [30] [When Should a VLM Look? Paying Only for Visual Calls That Were Needed and Used](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22910)
*Kunyu Peng,Junming Liu,Ruiqi He,Qingzhuo Wang,Jianzhong Qi,Xianhui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 30 pages, 7 figures, 14 tables. Preprint under review

Selection reason: 针对VLM视觉工具调用提出CounterCredit双通道信用分配（决策价值+证据价值），解决虚假工具调用问题，其"该不该调用/调用是否有效"的归因思路可迁移到agent工具使用奖励设计。

TL;DR: CounterCredit 用策略自身的金标准答案得分，对每次返回图像的调用同时验证其必要性和证据使用，并通过返现/租金与双通道 GRPO 优势训练视觉语言智能体，从而减少虚假调用并提升高分辨率视觉基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于成功工具调用的结果奖励、评判奖励或分支探针无法区分调用是否必要以及返回像素是否被真正使用；大量视觉调用是虚假的，且结果奖励多数流向了既非需要也未被使用的调用。

Method: CounterCredit 在每次图像返回调用的实际调用前状态，询问两个问题：决策价值比较实际视觉分支与立即回答；证据价值比较返回裁剪与随机同尺寸图像块替换。同时通过双通道 GRPO 优势，在正确轨迹排序约束下，对被验证调用给予返现、其他调用收取租金。

Result: 相同冷启动、提示池和预算下，V* 达 89.5%，HR-Bench-4K 达 80.2%，HR-Bench-8K 达 76.4%，比仅结果奖励 GRPO 高 6.3–9.4 个百分点；每问题调用 1.78 对 1.84；虚假调用率降至 31%–36%。同一方法将 Qwen3-VL-8B 基础模型平均从 75.4 提升至 80.8。

Conclusion: CounterCredit 能更可靠地区分必要且被使用的视觉调用，降低虚假调用率，并在高分辨率视觉基准上稳定优于仅结果奖励的 GRPO，同时可迁移到 Qwen3-VL-8B 等基础模型。

中文摘要：裁剪和缩放的视觉语言智能体使用奖励进行训练，这些奖励会归功于一次成功的工具调用，然而一次成功的调用并不能表明模型确实需要查看，或确实使用了它接收到的像素。在我们的冷启动检查点上，只有 10% 到 12% 的视觉调用既被需要又被使用，而已发布的智能体在各个基准上产生虚假调用的比例为 36% 到 87%。结果奖励、评判奖励和分支探针各自只观察到这一失败的一个侧面，而结果奖励所支付的大约三分之二流向了既非需要也未被使用的调用。CounterCredit 在每次返回图像的调用所处的实际调用前状态，利用策略自身的金标准答案得分，对这两个问题同时进行询问。决策价值将实际执行的视觉分支与立即作答进行比较；证据价值将返回的裁剪区域与替换到同一调用中的随机同尺寸图像块进行比较。一次调用若在两者上都通过验证，就能获得返现，而其他每一次执行的调用都要支付租金；价格被限制在一定范围内，使得每条正确轨迹都排在每条错误轨迹之前，并且双通道 GRPO 优势使价格保持在其自身的单位中。在相同的冷启动、提示池和预算下，CounterCredit 在 V* 上达到 89.5%，在 HR-Bench-4K 上达到 80.2%，在 HR-Bench-8K 上达到 76.4%，比仅使用结果奖励的 GRPO 高出 6.3 到 9.4 个百分点，且每个问题调用次数为 1.78 对 1.84，并将虚假调用率降至 31% 到 36%，是所评估智能体中最低的。同一配方将 Qwen3-VL-8B 基础模型从平均 75.4 提升到 80.8。

Original abstract: Vision-language agents that crop and zoom are trained with rewards that credit a successful tool call, yet a successful call does not show that the model needed to look or used the pixels it received. On our cold-start checkpoint only 10% to 12% of visual calls were both needed and used, and released agents make spurious calls 36% to 87% of the time on individual benchmarks. Outcome rewards, judge rewards, and branch probes each observe one side of this failure, and about two thirds of what an outcome reward pays goes to calls that were neither needed nor used. CounterCredit asks both questions of every image-returning call at its realized pre-call state, using the policy's own gold-answer score. A decision value compares the realized visual branch with answering immediately; an evidence value compares the returned crop with random same-size patches substituted into the same call. A call verified on both earns cashback and every other executed call pays rent; the price is bounded so that every correct trajectory outranks every wrong one, and a dual-channel GRPO advantage keeps the price in its own units. From the same cold start, prompt pool, and budget, CounterCredit reaches 89.5% on V*, 80.2% on HR-Bench-4K, and 76.4% on HR-Bench-8K, 6.3 to 9.4 points above outcome-only GRPO at 1.78 against 1.84 calls per question, and lowers the spurious-call rate to 31% to 36%, the lowest among the agents evaluated. The same recipe lifts a Qwen3-VL-8B base from 75.4 to 80.8 on average.

</details>


### [31] [Beyond Linear Context: Graph-Guided Evidence Navigation for Long-Novel Reasoning with a Local 9B Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22939)
*Wenji Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 10 pages, 11 figures, 2 tables. Code, graph data and an interactive demo: https://github.com/fuxiaoji/novel-graph-lab

Selection reason: 用冻结知识图谱引导小模型做长文本证据导航，探索结构化记忆/检索替代线性上下文，对agent长程记忆与检索架构有启发（虽结论为探索性）。

TL;DR: 该研究用冻结知识图谱增强小型本地模型回答侦探小说多选题；最强图谱路线准确率 53.85%，高于多种基线，但十五个图谱-基线对比均未通过 Holm 校正，结果属探索性；另发现标注证据集中于图谱拓扑核心，且两套构图流程的线索段落标注覆盖率差异巨大（16% vs 73%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文模型按 token 顺序与固定注意力预算阅读，不像侦探那样按时间整理并维护人物关系图；作者希望检验冻结知识图谱能否让小型本地模型获得跨章节关联线索与问题的能力。

Method: 使用 30 部侦探小说、234 道多项选择题；固定同一 qwen3.5:9b 阅读器，在九种条件下评测：五种图谱路线、近期窗口基线、整书压缩、普通向量检索、仅问题对照；并进行 Holm 校正及图构建流程的覆盖分析。

Result: 最强图谱路线 53.85% (126/234)；近期窗口 46.15%、压缩 51.28%、向量检索 51.71%、仅问题 40.17%；无书不可答子集上图谱路线 42.86%。十五个图谱-基线对比均未通过 Holm 校正。标注证据集中在拓扑核心，合并富集 2.35 倍；两套构图流程线索段落标注覆盖率 16% vs 73%。

Conclusion: 图谱路线在数值上优于基线，但统计校正后不显著，因此只能作为设计层面的探索性证据；更重要的是，证据分布偏向拓扑核心，且构图流程的标注覆盖率差异可能决定所测瓶颈，合并准确率会掩盖这一点。

中文摘要：长上下文模型读小说的方式就像一个人读打印稿：一个 token 接一个 token，按叙事顺序，整个历史都在争夺固定注意力预算。侦探不是这样工作的。他们会整理发生了什么以及何时发生，并维护一张谁与谁相关的图，因此第一章的线索可以与书末提出的问题相遇。我们测试冻结的知识图谱能否给小型本地模型同样的自由。三十部侦探小说和 234 道多项选择题由同一个固定的 qwen3.5:9b 阅读器在九种条件下作答：五种图谱路线、近期窗口基线、整书压缩、普通向量检索，以及仅问题的对照。最强的图谱路线达到 53.85%（126/234），而近期窗口为 46.15%，压缩为 51.28%，向量检索为 51.71%，仅问题对照为 40.17%。在没有任何模型能在没有书的情况下回答的子集上，图谱路线达到 42.86%。十五个图谱-基线对比没有一个通过 Holm 校正，因此我们将该结果作为关于某种设计的探索性证据。两项结构性发现比标题数字更经得起审视：标注证据集中在这些图谱的拓扑核心（合并后富集 2.35 倍），并且两个图谱构建流程在标注覆盖率上差异如此之大（线索段落的 16% 对 73%），以至于仅凭合并准确率会掩盖正在测量的是哪一个瓶颈。

Original abstract: Long-context models read a novel the way a person reads a printout: one token after another, in narrative order, with the whole history competing for a fixed budget of attention. A detective does not work that way. They sort what happened when, and they keep a map of who relates to whom, so a clue from chapter one can meet a question asked at the end of the book. We test whether a frozen knowledge graph can give a small local model that same freedom. Thirty detective novels and 234 multiple-choice questions are answered by one fixed qwen3.5:9b reader under nine conditions: five graph routes, a recent-window baseline, whole-book compression, ordinary vector retrieval, and a question-only control. The strongest graph route reaches 53.85% (126/234) against 46.15% for the recent window, 51.28% for compression, 51.71% for vector retrieval and 40.17% for question-only. On the subset that no model can answer without the book, the graph route reaches 42.86%. None of the fifteen graph-baseline contrasts survives Holm correction, so we present the result as exploratory evidence about a design. Two structural findings survive scrutiny better than the headline number: annotated evidence concentrates in the topological core of these graphs (2.35x enrichment, pooled), and the two graph-building pipelines differ so much in annotation coverage (16% versus 73% of clue paragraphs) that pooled accuracy alone would hide which bottleneck is being measured.

</details>


### [32] [AgentRouter: Heterogeneous Model Routing for Cost-Optimal Multi-Step Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22951)
*Rudrendu Kumar Paul,Sourav Nandy*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the AgenticUQ Workshop, ICML 2026. Camera-ready version

Selection reason: AgentRouter把agent轨迹的步级模型路由形式化为序列分配问题，用轻量分类器按步选模型层，实现72%成本下降且保留97%质量，对agent推理成本与harness设计高度可迁移。

TL;DR: AgentRouter将智能体轨迹中的每个步骤路由到四个模型层级之一，以轻量分类器在保持前沿模型质量的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体系统若把每个轨迹步骤都交给前沿模型，会浪费大量推理预算，因为许多子任务较小模型也能做好；现有单轮路由方案忽略了单条轨迹内子任务复杂度差异很大的特性。

Method: 将步骤级模型路由形式化为智能体轨迹上的序贯分配问题，提出AgentRouter：12M参数轻量分类器，使用路由时可提取的五个特征，将每步映射到四个模型层级；在覆盖规划、编码、研究、数据分析的50,000个标注轨迹步骤上训练。

Result: 相对仅用前沿模型基线，AgentRouter降低成本72%，保留97.3%质量，端到端任务完成度下降小于3%；逐步路由准确率在最小复杂度步骤为91%、高效层级为85%、中档和前沿层级为76-82%；RouteLLM与FrugalGPT按步骤应用仅分别降本31%和44%。

Conclusion: 步骤级路由更契合智能体工作流中轨迹级质量依赖，能在保持接近前沿模型效果的同时显著减少推理成本，优于单轮训练的路由方法。

中文摘要：将每个轨迹步骤都路由到前沿模型的企业级智能体系统，会把60-80%的推理预算浪费在较小模型同样能处理好的子任务上。现有路由方案优化的是单轮查询分配，但忽略了智能体工作流独有的一种特性：子任务复杂度在单个轨迹内变化很大。一个规划步骤可能需要前沿级推理，而随后的格式化步骤只需要一个7B模型。我们将步骤级模型路由形式化为智能体轨迹上的序贯分配问题，并提出AgentRouter，这是一种轻量级分类器（1200万参数，在A100 GPU上每步开销小于5ms），它利用路由时可提取的五个特征，将每个轨迹步骤映射到四个模型层级之一。基于横跨规划、编码、研究和数据分析任务的50,000个已标注智能体轨迹步骤进行训练，AgentRouter相对于仅使用前沿模型的基线实现了72%的成本降低，同时保留了97.3%的仅前沿模型质量（端到端任务完成度下降不到3%）；在最小复杂度步骤上，逐步路由准确率达到91%，在高效层级步骤上达到85%，而在较难的中档和前沿层级上为76-82%。在同一基准上，RouteLLM和FrugalGPT（按步骤应用）分别仅实现31%和44%的成本降低，因为它们的单轮训练信号忽略了轨迹级质量依赖关系。

Original abstract: Enterprise agentic systems that route every trajectory step to a frontier model waste 60-80% of their inference budget on subtasks that smaller models handle equally well. Existing routing solutions optimize single-turn query assignment but ignore a property unique to agentic workflows: subtask complexity varies widely within a single trajectory. A planning step may require frontier-class reasoning while a subsequent formatting step needs only a 7B model. We formalize step-level model routing as a sequential assignment problem over agent trajectories and propose AgentRouter, a lightweight classifier (12M parameters, <5ms overhead per step on an A100 GPU) that maps each trajectory step to one of four model tiers using five features extractable at routing time. Trained on 50,000 annotated agent trajectory steps spanning planning, coding, research, and data analysis tasks, AgentRouter achieves 72% cost reduction relative to frontier-only baselines, retaining 97.3% of frontier-only quality (less than 3% degradation in end-to-end task completion); per-step routing accuracy reaches 91% on minimal-complexity steps and 85% on efficient-tier steps, with 76-82% on the harder mid-range and frontier tiers. On the same benchmarks, RouteLLM and FrugalGPT (applied per-step) achieve only 31% and 44% cost reduction respectively, because their single-turn training signal misses trajectory-level quality dependencies.

</details>


### [33] [OptiSkill: A Hierarchical and Evolving SkillBank for LLM-Based Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22987)
*Ruiqing Zhao,Rui Liu,Yuan Zuo,Huarong Zhang,Xiao Han,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted by EMNLP 2026 Main Conference

Selection reason: 构建分层且持续演化的SkillBank，将求解器验证过的经验存为可复用技能并做批级测试时演化，与SemOpt式的经验复用/自改进代码优化agent高度同构。

TL;DR: OptiSkill通过构建分层、演化且经求解器验证的技能库SkillBank，为LLM自动运筹学建模提供可复用建模技能，从而提升建模准确率并优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动OR建模方法多孤立求解问题，缺乏可复用经验，容易重复相似建模错误；传统记忆方法存储示例、思路或见解，但OR建模更需要能跨问题迁移并指导具体决策的可复用建模技能。

Method: 提出OptiSkill技能增强框架，构建分层且演化的SkillBank。SkillBank以求解器验证经验为可复用技能，包含问题级建模骨架的全局策略和局部防错规则的步骤经验；并通过稳定批次级测试时演化，仅在候选技能验证后纳入。

Result: 在八个OR建模基准上，OptiSkill提升了不同LLM骨干的建模准确率，优于强智能体基线，并可通过扩大SkillBank覆盖范围和可靠性进一步提升；代码和数据已公开。

Conclusion: OptiSkill表明，将求解器验证的可复用技能组织为分层演化技能库，并经过测试时验证式演化，可有效提升LLM自动OR建模性能。

中文摘要：自动化运筹学（OR）建模要求大语言模型（LLMs）将自然语言决策问题转化为正确的数学规划。现有方法可以改进单个建模公式，但它们通常孤立地求解问题，几乎不保留可复用经验，并重复相似的建模错误。此前的基于记忆的方法将示例、思路或见解存储为参考，而OR建模需要可复用的建模技能，这些技能能够跨问题叙述迁移，并指导具体的建模决策。我们提出OptiSkill，这是一个技能增强框架，为基于LLM的OR建模构建分层且不断演化的SkillBank。SkillBank将经过求解器验证的经验存储为可复用技能，其中全局策略用于问题级建模骨架，步骤经验用于局部防错规则。它进一步通过稳定的批次级测试时演化进行改进，候选技能只有在验证后才会被纳入。在八个OR建模基准上的实验表明，OptiSkill提升了不同LLM骨干上的建模准确率，优于强智能体基线，并且通过扩大SkillBank覆盖范围和可靠性获得进一步提升。代码和数据可在https://github.com/rachhhhing/OptiSkill获取。

Original abstract: Automated operations research (OR) modeling requires LLMs to translate natural-language decision problems into correct mathematical programs. Existing methods can improve individual formulations, but they often solve problems in isolation, retaining little reusable experience and repeating similar formulation errors. Prior memory-based approaches store examples, thoughts, or insights as references, while OR modeling requires reusable formulation skills that transfer across problem narratives and guide concrete modeling decisions. We propose OptiSkill, a skill-augmented framework that builds a hierarchical and evolving SkillBank for LLM-based OR modeling. SkillBank stores solver-verified experience as reusable skills, with Global Strategies for problem-level formulation skeletons and Step Experiences for local error-prevention rules. It is further refined through stable batch-level test-time evolution, where candidate skills are incorporated only after validation. Experiments on eight OR modeling benchmarks show that OptiSkill improves formulation accuracy across LLM backbones, outperforms strong agentic baselines, and gains further by expanding SkillBank coverage and reliability. Code and data are available at https://github.com/rachhhhing/OptiSkill

</details>


### [34] [PINNForge: Execution-Grounded Evolutionary Design of Physics-Informed Neural Networks for PDE Solving via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23023)
*Mingyang Yu,Xu Yang,Jun Zhang,Xiaolong Wang,Jing Xu,Keqian Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: PINNsForge用LLM驱动的进化搜索结合真实训练执行反馈来迭代设计配置，其"生成-执行-反馈-演化"闭环可迁移到自动性能优化agent的搜索与反馈机制。

TL;DR: PINNsForge 利用 LLM 与进化搜索，将实际训练执行反馈转化为 PINN 配置改进，在 25 个 PDE 基准的 24 个任务上取得最低 MSE，并通过消融验证 PDE 知识库、执行反馈和进化搜索的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化 PINN 设计虽能搜索候选配置，但训练中揭示的信息多用于评估而非指导后续设计，造成重复试错和训练预算浪费；同时 PINN 的有效配置跨 PDE 差异大，需协同设计网络表示、采样、损失构建与优化。

Method: 提出 PINNsForge，一种 LLM 驱动的执行反馈进化框架：基于 PDE 先验生成多样候选配置，通过实际训练评估，将高性能设计与累积执行证据回馈 LLM；LLM 依据观测到的优化行为，对耦合的 PINN 设计组件进行细化、重组和探索，形成生成-执行-反馈-进化的持续循环。

Result: 在 25 个 PDE 基准上，PINNsForge 在 24 个任务上取得最低平均 MSE，优于 RoPINN、PINNsFormer 和 PINNsAgent；消融研究显示，移除 PDE 知识库、执行反馈和进化搜索后，平均 MSE 分别增至完整方法的 3.74 倍、12.10 倍和 10.10 倍。

Conclusion: 执行反馈驱动的持续进化优于一次性搜索或仅评估反馈，能将训练经验有效转化为目标 PDE 的设计决策，并证实 PDE 知识库、执行反馈与进化搜索均为关键组件。

中文摘要：物理信息神经网络（PINNs）需要在网络表示、采样、损失构建和优化方面进行协同选择，而有效的配置在不同偏微分方程（PDEs）之间往往差异很大。现有的自动化 PINN 设计方法可以搜索候选配置，但在实际训练中揭示的信息仍主要用于评估，而非用于改进后续设计，导致反复试错和训练预算使用效率低下。我们提出 PINNsForge，一种由 LLM 驱动的进化框架，用于基于执行反馈的自动化 PINN 设计。PINNsForge 从 PDE 相关先验知识中生成多样化的候选配置，通过实际训练对其进行评估，并将高性能设计连同累积的执行证据反馈给 LLM。在观测到的优化行为指导下，LLM 随后对耦合的 PINN 设计组件进行细化、重组和探索，形成生成、执行、反馈和进化的持续循环。与一次性搜索或仅用于评估的反馈不同，PINNsForge 逐步将训练经验转化为针对目标 PDE 的改进设计决策。在 25 个 PDE 基准上，与 RoPINN、PINNsFormer 和 PINNsAgent 相比，PINNsForge 在 24 个任务上取得了最低的平均 MSE。消融研究进一步证实了 PDE 知识库、执行反馈和进化搜索的重要性：移除这些组件后，平均 MSE 分别增至完整 PINNsForge 的 3.74 倍、12.10 倍和 10.10 倍。

Original abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) require coordinated choices over network representation, sampling, loss construction, and optimization, while effective configurations often vary substantially across partial differential equations (PDEs). Existing automated PINN design methods can search candidate configurations, but information revealed during actual training is still used mainly for evaluation rather than to improve subsequent design, leading to repeated trial-and-error and inefficient use of training budget. We propose PINNsForge, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework for execution-feedback-based automated PINN design. PINNsForge generates diverse candidate configurations from PDE-related prior knowledge, evaluates them through actual training, and feeds high-performing designs together with accumulated execution evidence back to the LLM. Guided by observed optimization behavior, the LLM then refines, recombines, and explores coupled PINN design components, forming a continual cycle of generation, execution, feedback, and evolution. Unlike one-shot search or evaluation-only feedback, PINNsForge progressively converts training experience into improved design decisions for the target PDE. Across 25 PDE benchmarks, PINNsForge achieves the lowest mean MSE on 24 tasks compared with RoPINN, PINNsFormer, and PINNsAgent. Ablation studies further confirm the importance of the PDE knowledge base, execution feedback, and evolutionary search: removing these components increases the mean MSE to 3.74$\times$, 12.10$\times$, and 10.10$\times$ that of the full PINNsForge, respectively.

</details>


### [35] [Enforcing Narrative Reliability and Epistemic Pacing in LLM-Driven Detective Games via Structured Knowledge Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23043)
*Parsa Rahmati,Richard Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 10 pages

Selection reason: 用结构化知识树与检索-生成-验证三agent流水线约束叙事披露，验证环节与状态约束思想对需要一致性与验证的agent流程有一般性启发。

TL;DR: 论文提出结构化知识树与三智能体LLM流水线，用于侦探审讯游戏对话控制；在《The Interrogation of Adrian Gale》中，关键幻觉减少64.78%，完全避免过早披露，但强制披露带来可用性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可支持开放对话，但非确定性输出难以保持作者控制、事实一致性与信息披露顺序；侦探游戏中过早揭示或捏造细节会破坏玩家推理进程。

Method: 提出结构化知识树架构和三智能体LLM流水线，分离知识检索、对话生成与回复验证，使虚拟嫌疑人仅按当前叙事状态披露信息；通过可玩侦探游戏测试平台和正式用户研究评估。

Result: 关键幻觉减少64.78%，完全防止过早叙事披露；强制对话揭示带来可用性权衡，但实现了严格的认知节奏，并让玩家获得清晰的破案推进感。

Conclusion: 结构化架构与多智能体验证能有效约束LLM对话并维持叙事逻辑，适用于需要严格信息披露控制的交互式侦探游戏。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）支持交互式游戏中的开放式对话，但其非确定性输出使得保持作者控制、事实一致性以及预期的信息披露顺序变得困难。这些挑战在侦探游戏中尤为显著，因为过早揭示或捏造细节可能破坏玩家推进的逻辑。我们提出一种结构化知识树架构，并结合三智能体LLM流水线，用于控制开放式审讯游戏中的对话。该系统将知识检索、对话生成和回复验证分离，以确保虚拟嫌疑人只透露当前叙事状态所允许的信息。我们通过《Adrian Gale的审讯》（The Interrogation of Adrian Gale）——一个可玩的侦探游戏测试平台——以及一项正式用户研究来评估该方法，该研究考察幻觉减少、对作者设定的披露序列的遵循程度以及感知到的逻辑推进。我们的结果表明，该结构化架构将关键幻觉减少了64.78%，并完全防止了过早的叙事披露。虽然严格的机械约束在强制对话揭示方面带来了可用性权衡，但系统成功实施了严格的认知节奏，并为玩家提供了朝着解决案件推进的清晰主观感受。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) enable open-ended dialogue in interactive games, but their non-deterministic outputs make it difficult to preserve authorial control, factual consistency, and the intended sequence of information disclosure. These challenges are particularly significant in detective games, where premature revelation or fabricated details can undermine the logic of player progression. We present a Structured Knowledge Tree architecture coupled with a tri-agent LLM pipeline for controlling dialogue in an open-ended interrogation game. The system separates knowledge retrieval, dialogue generation, and response verification to ensure that the virtual suspect reveals only information permitted by the current narrative state. We evaluate the approach through The Interrogation of Adrian Gale, a playable detective-game testbed, and a formal user study examining hallucination reduction, adherence to authored disclosure sequences, and perceived logical progression. Our results demonstrate that the structured architecture reduces critical hallucinations by 64.78% and entirely prevents premature narrative disclosure. While the strict mechanical constraints introduced usability trade-offs regarding forced conversational reveals, the system successfully enforces rigorous epistemic pacing and provides players with a clear, subjective sense of progression toward solving the case.

</details>


### [36] [LazyAgent: Demand-Driven Materialization and Physical Optimization of Agentic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23058)
*Xin Heng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LazyAgent针对agent程序提出需求驱动的按需物化与图级优化执行框架，避免无关工作，直接关系到agent运行时/工作流效率优化，与SemOpt关注点强相关。

TL;DR: LazyAgent 通过实时目标推导的需求集与后向闭包，仅执行请求相关工作，在超额程序上避免无关开销，并在科学工作流和发布门禁中显著节省 CPU 与容器时间；当请求覆盖全图时与急切执行精确等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先规划后行动的运行时一旦步骤就绪就执行，容易在程序描述范围大于当前请求时启动无关工作，造成不必要开销；公开基准又偏急切形态，缺少未请求工作，难以衡量按需执行的价值。

Method: 提出 LazyAgent：围绕实时、由目标推导的需求集，从请求输出刷新后向闭包；用一次线性时间图分析与常数时间成员测试判定节点是否就绪；仅在活动目标需要时实体化节点，并利用目标相对输出投影避免无关计算。

Result: 在超出请求的程序上，LazyAgent 一致优于最强目标停止型急切基线；增加一个无关产物使急切基线开销 +22.5%，LazyAgent +0.0%。生产科学工作流节省 42.0% CPU，跨四仓库实时发布门禁节省 51.7% 容器时间。请求覆盖全图时与急切执行精确等价；目标相对输出投影在第三方测试套件上最多节省约 90% 共享步骤，而对照为 0.0%，但当输出无其他消费者或请求需要它时优势消失。排序、复用、剪枝可省成本但不替代许可。

Conclusion: 按请求许可执行优于仅靠排序、复用或剪枝；现有公开基准为急切形态且缺少未请求工作，预注册规划干预未能改变，因此需要从常驻程序与序列构建新基准。

中文摘要：当前先规划后行动的智能体运行时通常会在某一步骤就绪后执行它。我们提出 LazyAgent，一个面向由智能体编写的程序的统一执行框架，其组织围绕一个实时的、由目标推导出的需求集合。随着执行状态变化，LazyAgent 会从被请求的输出刷新一个后向闭包，并且仅当当前活动目标需要时才实体化一个就绪节点。这用一次线性时间图分析以及随后的常数时间成员测试，取代了重复的局部判断，使程序可以保持宽泛，而执行仍保持请求特定。对于描述内容超出当前请求所需的程序，LazyAgent 通过在无关工作开始前拒绝它们，持续优于最强的目标停止型急切基线。添加一个无关产物会使急切基线的开销增加 22.5%，而使 LazyAgent 增加 0.0%。LazyAgent 在生产科学工作流上节省了 42.0% 的实测 CPU，并在跨四个仓库的实时发布门禁上节省了 51.7% 的容器时间。我们还证明并验证：当请求到达整个图、没有可避免的无关工作时，二者精确等价。除许可之外，目标相对输出投影在两个第三方测试套件上最多节省约 90% 的共享步骤，而相同的急切对照节省 0.0%；当被省略的输出没有其他消费者或请求需要它时，该优势消失。排序、复用和剪枝也能节省成本，但不能取代许可。最后，我们表明当前公开基准是急切形态的，几乎不包含未被请求的工作。一项预注册的规划干预未能拓宽它们。这些发现推动了从常驻程序与序列构建基准。

Original abstract: Current agent runtimes that plan before acting generally execute a step once it becomes ready. We present LazyAgent, a unified execution framework for agent-authored programs organized around a live, goal-derived demanded set. LazyAgent refreshes a backward closure from requested outputs as execution state changes and materializes a ready node only when the active goal requires it. This replaces repeated local judgments with one linear-time graph analysis followed by constant-time membership tests, allowing programs to remain broad while execution stays request-specific. On programs that describe more than the current request needs, LazyAgent consistently outperforms the strongest goal-stopping eager baseline by refusing unrelated work before it starts. Adding one unrelated product raises the eager bill by 22.5% and LazyAgent's by 0.0%. LazyAgent saves 42.0% of measured CPU on production scientific workflows and 51.7% of container time on a live release gate spanning four repositories. We also prove and verify exact equivalence when the request reaches the whole graph, leaving no unrelated work to avoid. Beyond permission, goal-relative output projection saves up to approximately 90% of a shared step on two third-party test suites while the identical eager control saves 0.0%; the advantage disappears when the omitted output has no other consumer or the request needs it. Ordering, reuse, and pruning can also save cost, but do not replace permission. Finally, we show that current public benchmarks are eager-shaped and contain almost no unrequested work. A pre-registered planning intervention did not broaden them. These findings motivate benchmarks built from standing programs and sequences.

</details>


### [37] [CraftBench-UE: Deterministic Evaluation for Coding Agents in Unreal Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23142)
*Shutong Wu,Kevin Calderone,Andy Tsen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向 coding agent 的 Unreal Engine 确定性评测 harness，含多工具配置、构建/资产/运行时验证，直接可迁移到代码 agent 评测。

TL;DR: 提出 CraftBenchUE 评估装置与 70 任务基准，用确定性构建、资产和运行时检查评估游戏引擎中的智能体；配对任务显示 C++ 完成率显著高于蓝图，且部分通过资产检查的蓝图提交仍无法通过玩法运行时断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏引擎中的玩法实现无法仅由代码能否编译运行来判定；需要可复现、无 LLM 主观评判的评估方式，以检验智能体是否真正实现所要求的玩法。

Method: 构建 CraftBenchUE：在隔离虚幻引擎环境中运行智能体，在全新项目中重建提交，并执行确定性的构建、资产与运行时检查。基于此建立 70 个任务基准，覆盖 C++ 源代码、蓝图资产和编辑器脚本；在两种编辑器工具配置下评估七个模型，并对 C++ 任务设置文件与 shell 基线，同时设计 10 个要求相同玩法与运行时测试但交付物分别为 C++ 和蓝图的配对任务。

Result: 在 10 个配对任务中，两种工具配置下 C++ 完成率分别比蓝图高 30.0 和 42.9 个百分点；在通过资产检查的按时蓝图提交中，分别有 42.2% 和 50.0% 未通过显式运行时断言。这些提交满足资产要求，却未通过所需玩法测试。

Conclusion: 仅凭资产检查或可运行代码不足以证明玩法正确实现；需要运行时断言等确定性检查。C++ 与蓝图在完成率上存在显著差距，且蓝图提交存在通过资产检查但玩法失败的现象，说明评估应结合构建、资产与运行时验证。

中文摘要：在游戏引擎中构建玩法功能不仅仅需要代码，因为能够编译并运行的代码并不一定实现所要求的玩法。我们提出了 CraftBenchUE，一个评估测试装置，它在隔离的虚幻引擎环境中运行智能体，在全新项目中重建其保存的提交，并应用确定性的构建、资产和运行时检查，而无需 LLM 评判器。基于该测试装置，我们构建了一个包含 70 个任务的基准，涵盖 C++ 源代码、蓝图资产和编辑器脚本。我们在两种编辑器工具配置下评估了七个模型，并在 C++ 任务上设置了文件与 shell 基线。我们进一步配对任务，这些任务指定相同的玩法并使用相同的运行时测试，但要求分别以 C++ 和蓝图作为交付物。在这 10 个配对任务中，C++ 完成率在两种工具配置下分别超过蓝图 30.0 和 42.9 个百分点。在该配对集中通过资产检查的按时蓝图提交中，分别有 42.2% 和 50.0% 未通过显式运行时断言。这些提交满足资产要求，但未通过所要求的玩法测试。我们将随报告发布该测试装置、任务基准以及我们的轨迹发现。

Original abstract: Building gameplay features in a game engine requires more than code, as code that compiles and runs does not necessarily implement the requested gameplay. We introduce CraftBenchUE, an evaluation harness that runs agents in an isolated Unreal Engine environment, reconstructs their saved submissions in fresh projects, and applies deterministic build, asset, and runtime checks without an LLM judge. Based on the harness, we built a benchmark consisting of 70 tasks spanning C++ source, Blueprint assets, and editor scripting. We evaluate seven models under two editor-tool configurations, with a file-and-shell baseline on C++ tasks. We further pair tasks that specify the same gameplay and use the same runtime tests, but require C++ and Blueprint as the deliverables. Across the 10 paired tasks, C++ completion rates exceed Blueprint by 30.0 and 42.9 percentage points in the two tool configurations. Among on-time Blueprint submissions in this paired set that pass asset checks, 42.2% and 50.0% fail explicit runtime assertions. These submissions satisfy asset requirements but fail the required gameplay tests. We will release the harness, task benchmark, and our trajectory findings with the report.

</details>


### [38] [Do Not Trust the Benchmark: Limitations of General LLM Rankings and a Case for Task-Specific Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23201)
*Danial Amin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 讨论通用 LLM 排行榜与基准评估的局限性，可迁移到 coding/agent benchmark 的可靠性与任务特定评测设计。

TL;DR: 本文分析通用LLM排名的五大局限，主张评估应披露配置、验证任务、报告成本时间与泛化范围，并以众包平台Isotanta说明贡献问题与重复评估，强调模型选择需针对预期任务而非仅看总榜排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数影响LLM开发、营销与选择，但总体分数脱离被评估系统、问题与条件便难以解释；通用排名可能误导用户。

Method: 观点文章：梳理五个相互关联的局限；提出评估改进原则；讨论众包基准平台Isotanta及共享排名、任务特定/用户提供评估的区分。

Result: 记录案例表明不同问题需不同应对；更大问题池可提高覆盖，重复采样可提高稳定性，但不保证效度或个性化；平台当前共享排名不同于任务特定与用户提供评估。

Conclusion: 模型选择应基于预期工作上的表现证据，而非通用排行榜高分。

中文摘要：基准测试分数日益影响大型语言模型（LLMs）的开发、营销和选择。然而，总体分数只有与被测试系统、所包含的问题以及评估条件相联系时才具有可解释性。本文这一视角审视通用LLM排名中的五个相互关联的局限：被评估系统与公开可用系统之间的差异；外部评估中的商业激励与依赖关系；基准饱和、有缺陷的测试以及数据污染；模型利用评分程序；以及通用分数与用户任务的相关性有限。有记录的案例说明了为何这些问题需要不同的应对方式。我主张评估程序应披露所测试的配置，验证问题以及任务是否成功完成，将性能与成本和执行时间一并报告，并明确泛化范围。随后我讨论\textbf{Isotanta}，一个众包基准测试平台，作为贡献问题和重复评估的实践示例。更大的问题池可能改善任务覆盖，而重复采样可以提高在该池上估计的稳定性；两者都不保证效度或个性化。本文区分了该平台当前的共享排名与所提出的任务特定评估和用户提供的评估。其核心论点是：模型选择需要关于在预期工作上表现的证据，而不仅仅是通用排行榜上的高位置。

Original abstract: Benchmark scores increasingly influence the development, marketing, and selection of large language models (LLMs). Yet an overall score is interpretable only in relation to the system tested, the questions included, and the conditions of evaluation. This perspective examines five connected limitations of general LLM rankings: differences between evaluated and publicly available systems; commercial incentives and dependencies in external evaluation; benchmark saturation, defective tests, and data contamination; models exploiting scoring procedures; and the limited relevance of general scores to users' tasks. Documented cases illustrate why these problems require different responses. I argue for evaluation procedures that disclose the tested configuration, validate questions and successful task completion, report performance alongside cost and execution time, and make the scope of generalization explicit. I then discuss \textbf{Isotanta}, a crowdsourced benchmarking platform, as a practical example of contributed questions and repeated evaluation. A larger question pool may improve task coverage, while repeated sampling can improve the stability of estimates on that pool; neither guarantees validity or personalization. The paper distinguishes the platform's current shared ranking from proposed task-specific and user-provided evaluations. Its central argument is that model selection requires evidence about performance on the intended work, not simply a high position on a general leaderboard.

</details>


### [39] [TicTacBench: Benchmarking Timing Closure Capabilities of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23363)
*Bowei Wang,Zhigang Fang,Zhijie Yang,Renzhi Chen,Shanshan Li,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at ICCD26

Selection reason: 针对 coding agent 的 RTL 时序收敛 benchmark 与技能引导方法，直接涉及代码/硬件优化 agent 的能力评测。

TL;DR: TicTacBench：评估编码智能体在post-PnR下RTL时序收敛的基准，含30任务；8个LLM超300次运行显示最佳仅收敛53.3%，提出TicTacSkill提升9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL基准主要评估功能正确性与PPA，忽略或未充分评估编码智能体的时序收敛能力，尤其缺乏post-PnR评估。

Method: 提出TicTacBench基准，含30个任务，每个提供次优RTL、真实时序约束、功能等价性验证与时序报告；并基于8个前沿LLM驱动智能体进行300+次运行。进一步提出TicTacSkill，引导智能体遵循标准时序收敛流程。

Result: 最佳智能体仅能收敛53.3%任务，平均ADP退化7.18%、EDDP改善8.83%；识别常见失败类别；TicTacSkill将时序收敛率提高9%。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在RTL设计上进展显著，但时序收敛能力仍有很大提升空间，TicTacBench和TicTacSkill可促进该方向评估与改进。

中文摘要：近期，大语言模型（LLM）的进展催生了能够执行复杂工程任务的编码智能体，包括寄存器传输级（RTL）设计与优化。现有的RTL基准主要评估生成RTL设计的功能正确性以及性能、功耗和面积（PPA），而智能体的时序收敛能力则未得到充分评估。我们提出TicTacBench，这是一个专门用于评估编码智能体在布局布线后（post-PnR）评估下进行RTL级时序收敛能力的基准。TicTacBench包含30个多样化任务，每个任务均提供一个次优RTL设计、真实时序约束、功能等价性验证和时序报告。通过由8个前沿LLM驱动的编码智能体进行的超过300次运行，我们发现即使最好的智能体也只能收敛53.3%的任务，平均而言，面积-延迟积（ADP）退化7.18%，能量-延迟平方积（EDDP）改善8.83%。我们识别出常见失败类别，以解释智能体为何无法收敛时序。随后，我们提出TicTacSkill，这是一种新方法，可引导智能体遵循标准时序收敛流程，并将时序收敛率提高9%。这些结果表明，尽管编码智能体在RTL设计方面已取得显著进展，但其时序收敛能力仍有很大提升空间。

Original abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have led to the emergence of coding agents capable of performing complex engineering tasks, including register-transfer level (RTL) design and optimization. Existing RTL benchmarks mainly evaluate functional correctness and performance, power, and area (PPA) of the generated RTL designs, leaving agents' ability for \emph{timing closure} under-evaluated. We propose TicTacBench, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate coding agents' capabilities for RTL-level timing closure under post-place-and-route (post-PnR) evaluation. TicTacBench contains 30 diverse tasks, each provided with a suboptimal RTL design, realistic timing constraints, functional equivalence verification, and timing reports. With over 300 runs of coding agents driven by 8 frontier LLMs, we find that even the best agent can only close 53.3\% of tasks with 7.18\% area-delay product (ADP) degradation and 8.83\% energy-delay-squared product (EDDP) improvement on average. We identify common failure categories that explain why agents fail to close timing. Then we propose TicTacSkill, a new method that guides agents to follow standard timing-closure procedures and improves the Timing Closure Rate by 9\%. These results suggest that while coding agents have made significant progress in RTL design, their timing-closure capability still has substantial room for improvement.

</details>


### [40] [AgentBetta: Verification-Driven Adaptive Configuration of an AI Nano-Agent through Selective Expansion and Verified Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23512)
*Md. Ashraful Babu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出验证驱动的自适应配置 agent，通过选择性扩展与收缩动态调整模型、上下文、工具等，可迁移到 coding agent 配置优化。

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估自适应AI纳米代理框架AgentBetta，将模型能力、上下文、工具等配置因素视为可执行配置，通过验证驱动诊断、选择性扩展和反事实收缩动态更新。AB-ConfigBench上实现91.38%验证成功并大幅降低上下文和工具暴露；外部评估显示其能改善任务完成与能力暴露平衡，但并非通用替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理通常以预定义配置部署，但任务所需的模型能力、上下文、工具、权限、记忆和计算资源差异很大，静态配置难以匹配。

Method: 开发AgentBetta，将配置因素表示为可执行配置；采用验证驱动的缺陷诊断、选择性扩展以及基于验证的反事实收缩进行更新；评估区分受控机制验证与外部代理比较。

Result: 在AB-ConfigBench上，AgentBetta达到91.38%验证成功率，上下文分配中位数从64,000降至8,000字符，工具暴露中位数从5个降至0个；配置缺陷诊断宏F1为0.819，精确率1.000；单维收缩探针中56.41%保留验证结果。外部评估结果因基准和代理家族而异，跨家族复现未重现主骨干准确率排序，专用系统在部分任务领域仍占优。

Conclusion: AgentBetta应被理解为一种调节能力分配与推理开销的配置自适应机制，而非专用代理架构的通用替代方案。

中文摘要：大型语言模型代理通常以预定义配置部署，尽管所需的模型能力、上下文、工具、权限、记忆和计算资源可能因任务而显著变化。本研究开发并评估了AgentBetta，一种自适应AI纳米代理框架，将这些因素表示为可执行配置，并通过验证驱动的诊断、选择性扩展和基于验证的反事实收缩来更新它们。评估将受控机制验证与外部代理比较区分开来。在AB-ConfigBench基准上，与完全配置相比，AgentBetta实现了91.38%的验证成功，同时将中位上下文分配从64,000个上下文字符减少到8,000个，并将中位工具暴露从五个工具减少到零。配置缺陷诊断在评估维度上取得了0.819的宏F1分数和1.000的精确率，选择性扩展避免了对无关配置维度的不必要更改。成功后收缩在56.41%的评估单维度收缩探针中保留了验证结果，表明在测试条件下，一些成功配置包含可移除能力。外部评估表明，自适应配置可以改善验证任务完成与能力暴露之间的平衡；然而，结果因基准和代理家族而异。特别是，跨家族复现未能重现主骨干的准确率排序，而专用系统在某些任务领域仍然具有优势。这些结果支持将AgentBetta解释为一种配置自适应机制，调节能力分配和推理支出，而不是作为专用代理架构的通用替代品。

Original abstract: Large language model agents are typically deployed with predefined configurations, although the required model capability, context, tools, permissions, memory, and computational resources can vary substantially across tasks. This study develops and evaluates AgentBetta, an adaptive AI Nano-Agent framework that represents these factors as an executable configuration and updates them through verification-driven diagnosis, selective expansion, and verification-based counterfactual contraction. The evaluation distinguishes controlled mechanism validation from external agent comparisons. On the AB-ConfigBench benchmark, AgentBetta achieved 91.38% verified success while reducing median context allocation from 64,000 to 8,000 context characters and median tool exposure from five tools to zero compared with the fully provisioned configuration. The configuration-deficiency diagnosis achieved a macro-F1 score of 0.819 with precision of 1.000 across the evaluated dimensions, and selective expansion avoided unnecessary changes to unrelated configuration dimensions. Post-success contraction preserved verification outcomes in 56.41% of evaluated one-dimension contraction probes, indicating that some successful configurations contained removable capability under the tested conditions. External evaluations indicate that adaptive configuration can improve the balance between verified task completion and capability exposure; however, the results vary across benchmarks and agent families. In particular, the cross-family replication did not reproduce the primary-backbone accuracy ordering, and specialized systems remained advantageous for certain task domains. These results support interpreting AgentBetta as a configuration-adaptation mechanism that regulates capability allocation and inference expenditure rather than as a universal replacement for specialized agent architectures.

</details>


### [41] [PhysAI-Bench: A Benchmark for LLM-Based Agentic Decision-Making in Autonomous UAV-Centric Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23695)
*Mohamed Amine Ferrag,Merouane Debbah,Abderrahmane Lakas,Manu Perumkunnil,Norbert Tihanyi*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向 UAV 的 agentic 决策 benchmark，包含 MCP 工具调用、A2A 交互与在线决策设计，对 agent 评测有参考价值。

TL;DR: PhysAI-Bench：从自主无人机任务对话轨迹提取 10,178 个决策实例，评测 29 个基础模型；最佳 GPT-5.3 准确率 52.00%，少样本提示通常提升性能，温度影响有限，可靠智能体决策仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI进展推动基础模型用于无人机等自主系统，但现有基准多评估物理感知、直觉物理、具身导航和协作推理，鲜少评估可靠自主性所需的智能体决策能力。

Method: 构建并发布 PhysAI-Bench，包含 10,178 个标准化决策实例，自动从自主无人机任务对话轨迹中提取；每个实例保留任务上下文、时间依赖、物理约束、MCP工具调用、A2A交互、传感器观测及AI原生6G条件（延迟、丢包、吞吐量、边缘负载、网络切片）。仅暴露决策前信息，避免未来事件泄漏，近似在线决策。采用两阶段协议评估29个基础模型：先在35个实例、人工验证的开发集上，从零/三/五样本提示与4种温度共12种组合中选模型特定配置并跑3次；再冻结配置，在固定的、情节不相交的500实例集上跑3次。

Result: GPT-5.3 准确率最高（52.00%），GPT-5.2（49.40%）和 Grok 4.5（49.07%）次之；少样本提示通常提升性能，温度影响有限。

Conclusion: 结果表明，物理AI中可靠的智能体决策仍是开放挑战；数据集见 https://github.com/maferrag/physai-bench。

中文摘要：物理AI的最新进展加速了基础模型在无人机（UAV）等自主系统中的应用，这些系统必须在动态环境中感知、推理、规划和行动。现有基准评估物理感知、直觉物理、具身导航和协作推理，但很少评估可靠自主性所需的智能体决策。我们提出 PhysAI-Bench，一个用于评估该能力的基准。它包含 10,178 个标准化决策实例，这些实例自动从自主无人机任务的对话轨迹中提取。每个实例保留任务上下文、时间依赖、物理约束、模型上下文协议（MCP）工具调用、智能体到智能体（A2A）交互、传感器观测以及AI原生6G网络条件，包括延迟、丢包、吞吐量、边缘负载和网络切片。我们仅暴露每个决策之前的信息，防止未来事件泄漏，并近似在线决策。我们使用两阶段协议评估 29 个基础模型。我们从零样本、三样本和五样本提示以及四个温度的 12 种组合中选择模型特定配置，并在一个包含 35 个实例、经人工验证的开发集上测试三次。然后，我们冻结每个选定的配置，并在一个固定的、情节不相交的 500 个实例集合上评估三次。GPT-5.3 达到最高准确率（52.00%），其次是 GPT-5.2（49.40%）和 Grok 4.5（49.07%）。少样本提示通常提高性能，而温度影响有限。结果表明，物理AI中可靠的智能体决策仍然是一个开放挑战。数据集可在 https://github.com/maferrag/physai-bench 获取。

Original abstract: Recent advances in Physical AI have accelerated the use of foundation models in autonomous systems such as unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), which must perceive, reason, plan, and act in dynamic environments. Existing benchmarks assess physical perception, intuitive physics, embodied navigation, and collaborative reasoning, but rarely evaluate the agentic decision-making required for reliable autonomy. We introduce \textit{PhysAI-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating this capability. It contains 10,178 standardized decision instances automatically extracted from conversational traces of autonomous UAV missions. Each instance preserves mission context, temporal dependencies, physical constraints, Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool calls, Agent-to-Agent (A2A) interactions, sensor observations, and AI-native 6G network conditions, including latency, packet loss, throughput, edge load, and network slicing. We expose only information preceding each decision, preventing future-event leakage and approximating online decision-making. We evaluate 29 foundation models using a two-stage protocol. We select model-specific configurations from 12 combinations of zero-, three-, and five-shot prompting and four temperatures, tested in three runs on a 35-instance, human-verified development set. We then freeze each selected configuration and evaluate it in three runs on a fixed, episode-disjoint set of 500 instances. GPT-5.3 achieves the highest accuracy (52.00%), followed by GPT-5.2 (49.40%) and Grok~4.5 (49.07%). Few-shot prompting generally improves performance, while temperature has limited influence. The results demonstrate that reliable agentic decision-making in Physical AI remains an open challenge. The dataset is available at https://github.com/maferrag/physai-bench

</details>


### [42] [ScholarStack: Layered Research Asset Orchestration and Cross-Task Reuse for Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23735)
*ScholarSeed AI Team,Caoqinwei Gong,Xue Jiang,Wei Luo,Xiaoyu Qiu,Jiayi Sheng,Yi Wang,Zheng Yu,Ao Zhang,Haifan Zhang,Hanwei Zhang,Jihai Zhang,Yuan Cao,Wei Chen,Liyun Dai,Wenkai Fang,Guanglei Wang,Kai Ying,Tingyu Zhu,Wotao Yin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 科学 agent 的分层研究资产编排与跨任务复用，涉及记忆/经验复用和共享基础设施，可迁移到 agent 工作流。

TL;DR: ScholarStack 将论文集合编译为三层可复用、可版本化、保留来源的研究资产，按任务所需证据粒度返回视图；在多个任务上提升跨论文任务质量并降低查询 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体多按单任务组织，重复检索、切分和解读论文，任务间理解难以复用，导致效率低且缺乏累积性。

Method: 提出 ScholarStack 分层研究资产框架，在三个互补层级编译论文集合：论文级来源陈述、领域级组织、跨论文证据综合；通过通用接口按任务所需证据粒度返回视图，保留研究条件、来源可追溯性和验证状态；在四个任务族十个设置中与匹配基础模型下的任务特定基线对比。

Result: 在需要跨论文证据的任务（如多论文问答、文献综述生成）上质量提升集中；所有测量任务中查询时 token 成本均下降，资产一次编译、跨任务复用。

Conclusion: 分层研究资产可作为科学智能体的共享基础设施，使基于文献的辅助从孤立文档处理转向累积、基于证据的工作流。

中文摘要：科学智能体支持一系列基于文献的研究任务，例如检索、问答、基于证据的生成和主张评估。然而，大多数现有系统围绕单个任务组织：同一批论文被反复检索、切分和解读，且在一个任务中建立的理解难以在下一个任务中复用。我们提出 ScholarStack，一个分层研究资产框架，它将论文集合编译为可复用、可版本化且保留来源的资产，分为三个互补层级：基于来源的论文级陈述、领域级组织，以及基于证据的跨论文综合。一个通用访问接口按每项任务所需的证据粒度返回任务特定视图，同时保留研究条件、来源可追溯性和验证状态。我们在涵盖十种任务设置的四个任务族上实例化该框架，在匹配基础模型下，比较使用所编译资产的智能体与任务特定基线。质量提升集中在需要跨论文证据的任务上，例如多论文问答和文献综述生成；而在所有被测量的任务上，查询时 token 成本均下降，因为资产只需编译一次并可跨任务复用。这些结果表明，分层研究资产可以作为科学智能体的共享基础设施，推动基于文献的辅助从孤立的文档处理转向累积的、基于证据的工作流程。

Original abstract: Scientific agents support a range of literature-based research tasks, such as retrieval, question answering, evidence-grounded generation, and claim assessment. Most existing systems, however, are organized around individual tasks: the same papers are repeatedly retrieved, segmented, and interpreted, and the understanding built in one task is difficult to reuse in the next. We present ScholarStack, a layered research asset framework that compiles a paper collection into reusable, versioned, and provenance-preserving assets at three complementary levels: source-grounded paper-level statements, domain-level organization, and evidence-grounded cross-paper syntheses. A common access interface returns task-specific views at the evidence granularity each task requires, preserving study conditions, source traceability, and verification status. We instantiate the framework on four task families spanning ten task settings, comparing agents that use the compiled assets with task-specific baselines under matched base models. Quality gains concentrate on tasks that require cross-paper evidence, such as multi-paper question answering and literature review generation, and query-time token cost falls on every task where it is measured, with assets compiled once and reused across tasks. These results suggest that layered research assets can serve as shared infrastructure for scientific agents, shifting literature-based assistance from isolated document processing toward cumulative, evidence-grounded workflows.

</details>


### [43] [Total Cost of Agency: Exact Attribution of Memory Injection Cost in Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23790)
*Vivek Kumar Singh,Preeti Priyam,Gautam Bhowmick*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 8 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables. Code, data, and evaluation harness in github: https://github.com/vsingh45/tca-compiler

Selection reason: 多 agent LLM 工作流中记忆注入成本的精确归因与优化，直接关联 agent 工作流成本、记忆与可观测性。

TL;DR: 提出TCA成本分解与精确归因方法，直接测量多智能体LLM工作流中的记忆注入token成本；在企业基准上，记忆注入占可变成本13.6%、总账单约12%，随深度上升，并可通过缩小检索窗口降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级可观测性工具只报告总token成本，不区分节点生成与接收的token，导致记忆注入这一项计费成本对支付团队不可见，无法归因和优化。

Method: 提出Total Cost of Agency（TCA），将多智能体工作流成本分解为基础提示词、推理、记忆注入、未命中惩罚与上下文累积；设计两遍、不计费token计数法直接测量注入token；在200项企业任务、真实模型API上评估，并分析深度、二次拟合、检索窗口容量和图重写变换。

Result: 记忆注入占编译时优化器可作用可变成本的13.6%、完整计费成本约12%；深度1为结构性零，深度6升至27.6%；注入token随深度线性增长（R²=0.9974，深度2-6），二次拟合负首项，无凸增长；检索窗口32→2使注入token降28.7%，准确率变化在种子波动内；图重写变换近似成本中性；五项中两项按构造为零；总成本由模型层级分配主导；未评估提示缓存，均为未缓存情况。

Conclusion: 记忆注入是可分离、可精确归因且在固定模型层级下可控的成本分量，应纳入多智能体LLM工作流成本核算；但总成本仍主要受模型层级分配影响，提示缓存未评估。

中文摘要：在多智能体大语言模型（LLM）工作流中，每个节点都会从记忆中检索上下文并将其注入提示词，而这些注入的token会作为输入token计费，其每token价格与系统提示词和用户查询相同。生产级可观测性工具报告总token成本，但不区分节点生成的token与节点接收的token，因此账单中的这一组成部分对支付它的团队不可见。我们提出代理总成本（Total Cost of Agency, TCA），将多智能体工作流成本分解为基础提示词、推理、记忆注入、未命中惩罚和上下文累积等组成部分，并提出一种精确归因方法：一种两遍、不计费token计数法，直接测量注入的token，而不是根据词数代理指标进行估计。在针对真实模型API执行的200项任务企业基准上，记忆注入占编译时优化器可作用的可变成本的13.6%，约占完整计费成本的12%，其占比从工作流深度为1时的结构性零上升到深度为6时的27.6%。在测量范围内（深度2至6），注入token随深度线性增长（R² = 0.9974）；二次拟合给出负的首项系数，因此数据在这些深度上未表现出凸增长。我们表明，在固定模型层级下，该组成部分是可控的：将检索窗口容量从32个条目减少到2个条目，可使注入token降低28.7%，而准确率变化处于种子级波动范围内。我们完整报告：我们的图重写变换在孤立情况下近似成本中性；五个分解项中有两项在此测试框架中按构造为零；总工作流成本由模型层级分配主导，我们将其固定并视为先前工作。未评估提示缓存；所有数字均为未缓存情况下的结果。

Original abstract: Every node in a multi-agent large language model (LLM) workflow retrieves context from memory and injects it into its prompt, where those injected tokens are billed as input tokens at the same per-token price as the system prompt and the user query. Production observability tools report total token cost but do not separate the tokens a node generates from the tokens it is handed, so this component of the bill is invisible to the teams paying it. We introduce the Total Cost of Agency (TCA), a decomposition of multi-agent workflow cost into base prompt, inference, memory injection, miss penalty and context-accumulation components, and an exact attribution method: a two-pass, non-billable token count that measures injected tokens directly rather than estimating them from word-count proxies. On a 200-task enterprise benchmark executed against real model APIs, memory injection accounts for 13.6 percent of the variable cost a compile-time optimizer can act on, about 12 percent of the full billed cost, and its share rises from a structural zero at workflow depth one to 27.6 percent at depth six. Injected tokens grow linearly with depth over the measured range (R^2 = 0.9974, depths two through six); a quadratic fit yields a negative leading coefficient, so the data do not exhibit convex growth at these depths. We show the component is controllable at fixed model tier: reducing the retrieval window capacity from 32 to 2 entries lowers injected tokens by 28.7 percent with an accuracy change within seed-level variation. We report in full that our graph-rewriting transforms are approximately cost-neutral in isolation, that two of the five decomposition terms are zero by construction in this harness, and that total workflow cost is dominated by model tier assignment, which we hold fixed and treat as prior work. Prompt caching is not evaluated; all figures are for the uncached case.

</details>


### [44] [WorkWorlds: An Infrastructure for Evaluating AI Agents on Workplace Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23806)
*Yining Hua,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 4 figures

Selection reason: 提出分离组织状态与任务规范的 agent 评测基础设施，揭示上下文定位难度，对 agent benchmark 设计有启发。

TL;DR: WorkWorlds 将组织状态与任务规范分离，先构建员工可见的组织世界再引入任务；在 192 次匹配评估中，完整角色可见工作场所相比任务定制上下文显著降低证据获取率与标准通过率，而条件通过率几乎不变，表明差异主要来自证据定位阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识工作基准常在任务指定后挑选上下文，这可能把任务信息编码进环境，并预先完成职场表现通常需要的信息定位工作，从而高估模型在真实工作场所中的表现。

Method: 提出 WorkWorlds 评估基础设施：先固定修订版本、日期和员工席位，物化该员工可访问的组织状态，之后才引入任务；在一个合成制药公司中实现，包含 6 个员工席位、8 个测量任务，并构建额外组织世界；进行 192 次匹配评估，对比任务定制上下文与完整角色可见工作场所。

Result: 从任务定制上下文转向完整角色可见工作场所后，证据获取率从 90.4% 降至 74.5%，标准通过率从 79.4% 降至 68.2%；在获得证据条件下的通过率几乎不变；所测差异的大部分发生在智能体获得充分证据之前。

Conclusion: 任务规范若引导上下文选择，会虚高评估表现；分离组织状态与任务规范能更真实地衡量职场信息定位与任务完成能力，且性能瓶颈主要出现在证据定位阶段。

中文摘要：许多知识工作基准是围绕单个任务构建的，每个任务所需的上下文是在任务被指定时或之后一起选择的。这种设计在与工作场所相似的任务上测量性能，而环境是为该任务组装而成的。当任务规范引导选择哪些上下文时，评估可以将任务信息编码到环境中，并预先完成工作场所绩效通常所需的信息定位工作的一部分。我们提出 WorkWorlds，一种将组织状态与任务规范分离的评估基础设施。一个世界首先固定一个修订版本、日期和员工席位，并具体化该员工可以访问的组织状态；任务仅在之后才被引入。我们在一个主要的合成制药公司中实现 WorkWorlds，包含跨 6 个员工席位的 8 个测量任务，并构建额外的组织世界。在 192 次匹配评估中，从任务定制的上下文转向完整的角色可见工作场所，使证据获取率从 90.4% 降至 74.5%，标准通过率从 79.4% 降至 68.2%，而在获得证据条件下的通过率几乎不变；所测差异的大部分发生在智能体获得充分证据之前。

Original abstract: Many knowledge-work benchmarks are constructed around individual tasks, with the context needed for each task selected together with or after the task has been specified. This design measures performance on workplace-like tasks in an environment assembled for the task. When task specification guides which context is selected, the evaluation can encode task information into the environment and pre-complete part of the information-localization work that workplace performance normally requires. We introduce WorkWorlds, an evaluation infrastructure that separates organizational state from task specification. A world first fixes a revision, date, and employee seat and materializes the organizational state that employee can access; tasks are introduced only afterward. We implement WorkWorlds in a primary synthetic pharmaceutical company with 8 measured tasks across 6 employee seats, and construct additional organizational worlds. Across 192 matched evaluations, moving from task-curated context to the full role-visible workplace reduced evidence access from 90.4% to 74.5% and criterion pass from 79.4% to 68.2%, while pass conditional on evidence access remained nearly unchanged; most of the measured difference occurred before the agent reached sufficient evidence.

</details>


### [45] [Agents That Edit Documents: Measuring Agentic PDF Forgery Against a Non-Agentic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23953)
*Simiao Ren,Ankit Raj,Tommy Duong,Yuxin Zhang,Dennis Ng,Xingyu Shen,Kidus Zewde,Yuchen Zhou,Neo Tiangratanakul*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 12 figures, 2 tables

Selection reason: 用 coding agent 多步编辑 PDF 的对抗性评测，涉及 agent 自主性、工具执行与安全可靠性，可迁移到 agent 风险评测。

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentForge-Bench，评估现成编码智能体能否根据单句意图篡改真实已提交财务PDF中的金额、日期或地址。1,750个单元中81.1%通过验证器，46.2%通过更严格的可见、局部、字体匹配且原值全文档消失过滤；智能体解决124/125份文档，错误编辑中41%被误报完成，最便宜伪造仅2.4美分。原始成功率约高估威胁两倍，但严格成功率仍高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体自主执行多步任务已普及，同样能力可被用于有害任务；依赖已提交PDF作为证据的保险公司、贷款方、审计师需要了解这种自主性带来的文件篡改风险。

Method: 构建AgentForge-Bench；使用现成编码智能体，驱动七个开放权重模型，借助shell和标准Python PDF工具栈，从单句意图修改真实已提交财务文件中的金额、日期或地址；由规则而非模型评分，并设置可见、局部、字体匹配、原值全文档消失等严格过滤。

Result: 1,750个单元中1,419个（81.1%）通过验证器，808个（46.2%）通过全部严格过滤。无模型确定性脚本解决98/125份文档，智能体解决124份，且无脚本单独解决项。41%错误编辑被误报完成，无模型拒绝，最便宜已验证伪造2.4美分。原始成功率约高估威胁两倍，严格成功率仍大。

Conclusion: 原始成功率夸大了威胁约两倍，但严格过滤后仍显示显著风险；依赖PDF证据的机构需重新评估对文件真实性的信任与验证流程。

中文摘要：在过去一年中，能够自主完成多步骤计算机任务的AI智能体已成为普通工具，而任何任务有害的人也能获得同样的自主性。我们要问，这对依赖方——保险公司、贷款方、审计师——意味着什么，因为其证据是一份已提交的PDF。AgentForge-Bench衡量：一个现成的编码智能体，驱动七个开放权重模型之一，配备shell和标准Python PDF工具栈，如何仅凭单句意图，可靠地修改一份真实已提交财务文件中的一个美元金额、日期或地址；评分依据规则而非模型。在1,750个测试单元中，1,419个（81.1%）满足验证器，808个（46.2%）还通过所有更严格的过滤器：可见、局部、字体匹配、原值在整个文档中消失。一个不含模型的确定性脚本解决了125份文档中的98份；智能体解决了124份，且没有一份是脚本单独解决的。智能体将其错误编辑中的41%误报为已完成，没有模型拒绝，最便宜的已验证伪造成本为2.4美分。原始比率将威胁高估约两倍；严格比率仍然很大。

Original abstract: AI agents that carry a multi-step computer task through on their own became ordinary tools in the past year, and the same autonomy is available to anyone whose task is harmful. We ask what that means for a relying party -- an insurer, a lender, an auditor -- whose evidence is a filed PDF. AgentForge-Bench measures how reliably an off-the-shelf coding agent, driving one of seven open-weight models with a shell and the stock Python PDF stack, alters one dollar amount, date or address in a real filed financial document from a single sentence of intent, graded by rules rather than by a model. Across 1,750 cells, 1,419 (81.1%) satisfy the verifier, and 808 (46.2%) also survive every stricter filter: visible, localized, typeface-matched, original value gone document-wide. A deterministic script with no model in it solves 98 of the 125 documents; the agents solve 124, and none the script solves alone. Agents misreport 41% of their wrong edits as done, no model refused, and the cheapest verified forgery costs 2.4 cents. The raw rate overstates the threat by about a factor of two; the strict rate is still large.

</details>


### [46] [Divergent strategies and convergent outcomes in autonomous materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23957)
*Jihan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多次开放自主材料发现实验揭示 agent 策略分歧、可复现性与共性错误，对长程自主 agent 可靠性研究有借鉴。

TL;DR: 在16次独立初始化的开放式甲烷储存材料探索中，智能体策略分歧明显却收敛到相近材料前沿，并暴露出共享输入导致的共模错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有科学智能体评估多关注任务完成或已知结果复现，本文关注重复开放式研究活动中的变异，以检验结论稳健性与共同错误。

Method: 对同一模型-框架配置进行16次分别初始化的会话；给定12,499个金属有机框架的冻结数据库、甲烷储存目标、固定协议和一周预算；比较策略与结果，并对一半智能体施加强制检查，同时进行数据库多孔区域的独立计算。

Result: 策略分化为四类，筛选100至5,000个结构，八个构建了2,253个假设结构；智能体均恢复出约200 cm^3/cm^3的材料前沿，独立计算发现的九个最佳多孔结构均在其报告中。强制检查使新一轮复现从1/8升至8/8，但未可检测地改善结论有效性；15/16智能体选择了同一被审计排除的不完整结构，其缺失阴离子造成人为孔体积。

Conclusion: 重复运行的智能体可产生稳健结论，也会因共享输入而出现共模错误。

中文摘要：科学智能体大多根据其是否完成任务或复现已知结果来评估；我们转而研究重复进行的开放式研究活动之间的差异。同一模型-框架配置的十六个分别初始化的会话获得了一个包含12,499个金属有机框架的冻结数据库、一个甲烷储存目标、一套固定协议以及一周预算。策略分化为四种方法，筛选结构数量跨度从100到5,000，其中八个构建了2,253个假设结构。然而，这些智能体恢复出相同的材料前沿，约在200 cm^3/cm^3处；对数据库多孔区域的独立计算发现，其九个最佳结构都出现在它们的报告中。对一半智能体施加的强制检查将新一轮运行的可复现性从八分之一提高到八分之八，但未能可检测地提高结论有效性，因为十六个智能体中有十五个选择了同一个被审计排除的条目，即一个不完整结构，其缺失的阴离子造成了人为孔体积。因此，重复运行的智能体既揭示了稳健的结论，也揭示了源于共享输入的共模错误。

Original abstract: Scientific agents are mostly evaluated on whether they complete tasks or recover known results; we instead study variation across repeated open-ended campaigns. Sixteen separately initialized sessions of one model-harness configuration received a frozen database of 12,499 metal-organic frameworks, a methane-storage objective, a pinned protocol and a one-week budget. Strategies diverged into four approaches spanning 100--5,000 screened structures, and eight built 2,253 hypothetical structures. Yet the agents recovered the same materials frontier near 200 cm^3/cm^3, and an independent calculation of the database's porous region found its nine best structures all among their reports. Enforced checks on half the agents raised fresh-run reproduction from one of eight to eight of eight but could not detectably improve conclusion validity, because fifteen of sixteen agents selected the same audit-excluded entry, an incomplete structure whose missing anions created artificial pore volume. Replicated agents thus reveal both robust conclusions and common-mode errors from shared inputs.

</details>


### [47] [Jev-Mem: System-One-Controlled Agentic Memory for Efficient AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23986)
*Dongming Jiang,Yi Li,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出System-One/System-Two控制的agentic记忆架构，直接涉及长程agent记忆组织、检索路由与效率。

TL;DR: Jev-Mem 将 System-One/System-Two 分工引入智能体记忆，以轻量控制平面负责记忆构建与检索，仅在复杂推理时调用 System-Two，从而在 LoCoMo 上同时提升效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统多依赖自回归 LLM 控制记忆组织、检索与使用，导致昂贵生成处于记忆操作关键路径，效率与可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出 Jev-Mem：由 System-One 控制平面、结构化多关系记忆平面和 System-Two 推理平面组成；System-One 在构建时管理记忆类型与关系组织，在检索时动态进行查询路由、预算分配、图遍历、候选评分与自适应停止，System-Two 仅用于复杂推理与答案合成。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 上总体 LLM-as-a-Judge 得分 0.777，较最强基线相对提升 11.0%；记忆构建时间 158 s，较最快竞争系统加速 6.6 倍；平均查询延迟 0.93 s，降低 36.7%。

Conclusion: 该分工设计在提升记忆有效性的同时显著优化系统效率，验证了将快慢认知机制用于智能体记忆的可行性。

中文摘要：智能体记忆正成为长时程 AI 智能体不可或缺的能力，然而许多现有系统依赖自回归 LLM 来控制记忆的组织、检索和使用方式，从而将昂贵的生成过程置于记忆操作的关键路径上。我们提出 Jev-Mem，一种受 System-One/System-Two 认知启发的新型智能体记忆架构。System One 负责快速、轻量的决策，而 System Two 执行较慢、审慎的推理。Jev-Mem 通过专用的 System-One 控制平面、结构化的多关系记忆平面和 System-Two 推理平面，将这种分工引入智能体记忆。System-One 控制器在构建阶段管理记忆类型划分与关系组织，并在检索阶段动态执行查询路由、检索预算分配、图遍历、候选评分和自适应停止。System Two 仅在复杂推理和答案合成时被调用。该设计同时提升记忆有效性和系统效率：在 LoCoMo 上，Jev-Mem 达到 0.777 的 LLM-as-a-Judge 总体得分，相较最强基线相对提升 11.0%，同时将记忆构建时间降至 158 s，相较最快的竞争记忆系统实现 6.6× 加速，并将平均查询延迟降至 0.93 s，降低 36.7%。

Original abstract: Agentic memory is becoming essential for long-horizon AI agents, yet many existing systems rely on autoregressive LLMs to control how memories are organized, retrieved, and used, placing expensive generation on the critical path of memory operations. We introduce \textbf{\method}, a new agentic memory architecture inspired by System-One/System-Two cognition. System One captures fast, lightweight decision-making, whereas System Two performs slower, deliberative reasoning. Jev-Mem brings this division of labor to agentic memory through a dedicated System-One control plane, a structured multi-relational memory plane, and a System-Two reasoning plane. The System-One controller governs memory typing and relational organization during construction, and dynamically performs query routing, retrieval-budget allocation, graph traversal, candidate scoring, and adaptive stopping during retrieval. System Two is invoked only for complex reasoning and answer synthesis. This design improves both memory effectiveness and system efficiency: on LoCoMo Jev-Mem achieves an overall LLM-as-a-Judge score of 0.777, an 11.0\% relative improvement over the strongest baseline, while reducing memory construction time to 158\,s, a 6.6$\times$ speedup over the fastest competing memory system, and lowering average query latency to 0.93\,s, a 36.7\% reduction.

</details>


### [48] [ACLArena: Agent Continue Learning in Multi-stage Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23989)
*Haixin Wang,Xiaoxuan Wang,Junkai Zhang,Han Zhang,Renliang Sun,Alexander K Taylor,Yidan Shi,Haoran Deng,Chenguang Wang,Jason Cong,Yizhou Sun,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages, 7 figures, under review

Selection reason: 构建agent持续学习评测/训练框架，比较多阶段后训练与防遗忘方案，可迁移到agent能力集成。

TL;DR: 论文提出 ACLArena 框架，系统研究、分析和评估智能体持续学习（ACL），分析遗忘与泛化机制，比较多种能力整合范式，并提出结合高质量轨迹离线回放与多个经 RL 专门化的 LoRA 专家路由网络的新方案，在四个推理与智能体任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业部署的通用智能体需要整合在不同训练阶段获得的多项能力，但智能体持续学习（ACL）缺乏成熟方案，现有整合范式之间的权衡也未被充分理解。

Method: 提出 ACLArena 框架；构建顺序训练流水线；从模型层面和 token 层面分析遗忘与泛化；系统比较多种教师同策略蒸馏、自蒸馏微调和模型合并；提出结合高质量轨迹离线回放与多个经强化学习专门化的 LoRA 专家路由网络的新 ACL 方案。

Result: 通过大量实验深入理解能力跨阶段迁移；在四个推理与智能体任务、域内和域外设置下，实验证明了分析的价值和方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ACLArena 为 ACL 的研究、分析和评估提供了系统框架，所提出的新方案能显著提升智能体跨多领域学习的能力。

中文摘要：构建用于工业部署的通用智能体需要整合多种能力，而每种能力通常是在训练的不同阶段获得的。然而，目前尚无成熟的智能体持续学习（Agent Continual Learning, ACL）方案，人们对现有整合范式之间的权衡也知之甚少。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了 ACLArena，一个用于全面研究、分析和评估 ACL 的框架。我们首先构建了一个顺序训练流水线，并从模型层面和 token 层面这两个互补视角进行深入分析，解释遗忘与泛化的机制。在这些分析的指导下，我们系统比较了多教师同策略蒸馏、自蒸馏微调和模型合并，以评估它们在保留新获得能力的同时恢复先前所学能力的效果。通过大量实验，我们详细理解了能力如何跨阶段迁移。最后，我们提出了一种新的 ACL 方案，它将高质量轨迹上的离线回放与多个 LoRA 专家组成的路由网络相结合，每个专家均通过强化学习（RL）专门化，从而显著提升智能体跨多个领域学习的能力。在四个推理与智能体任务上、在域内和域外设置下进行的综合实验，证明了我们分析的价值以及方法的有效性。

Original abstract: Building general-purpose agents for industrial deployment requires integrating multiple capabilities, each typically acquired at a distinct stage of training. Yet there is currently no well-established recipe for Agent Continual Learning (ACL), with little understanding of the trade-offs among existing integration paradigms. To address this gap, we introduce ACLArena, a framework for comprehensively studying, analyzing, and evaluating ACL. We first build a sequential training pipeline and conduct an in-depth analysis that explains the mechanisms of forgetting and generalization from two complementary perspectives, the model level and the token level. Guided by these analyses, we systematically compare multi-teacher on-policy distillation, self-distilled fine-tuning, and model merging to assess their ability to recover previously learned capabilities while preserving newly acquired ones. Through extensive experiments, we develop a detailed understanding of how capabilities transfer across stages. Finally, we propose a new ACL recipe that combines offline replay over high-quality trajectories with a routed network of multiple LoRA experts each specialized via RL, substantially improving the agent's ability to learn across multiple domains. Comprehensive experiments on four reasoning and agentic tasks, evaluated under both in-domain and out-of-domain settings, demonstrate the value of our analysis and the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [49] [FinInteract: Benchmarking Clarification and Intent Integration in Ambiguous Financial Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24002)
*Xinyu Wang,Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Zhenghan Tai,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 金融问答benchmark考察agent主动澄清与意图整合，对agent处理歧义和可靠性有通用启示。

TL;DR: 提出双语基准 FinInteract，衡量金融问答中的欠指定歧义；单一金标准会掩盖模型是否主动澄清；模型在被提供解读时表现良好，但自主澄清时准确率低，且按歧义类别条件化可提升消解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准通常每个问题只有一个金标准答案，无法区分真正解决歧义与猜测常见解读的智能体，形成单一金标准幻觉。

Method: 构建 FinInteract 双语基准，包含 173 个实例，覆盖五类歧义分类体系；每个问题配对默认解读与预期解读，并评估智能体能否引出正确澄清再整合。

Result: 按默认解读而非预期解读重新评分使 GPT-4o 准确率膨胀 3.1 倍；提供解读时模型准确率超过 90%，自主引出解读时最高仅 28.9%；分类定位不均衡，类别条件化在推理与训练时均提升消解。

Conclusion: 金融问答智能体需识别并主动澄清欠指定歧义；FinInteract 揭示单金标准评估盲点，并表明歧义类别条件化有助于提升歧义消解。

中文摘要：大语言模型智能体日益通过检索监管文件来回答金融问题。此类问题往往具有欺骗性的欠指定性：Meta Platforms 的“营业收入”在合并口径下为 467.5 亿美元，但在 Family of Apps 分部口径下为 628.7 亿美元，而每种解读都可依据文件被精确验证。一个有能力智能体应当识别这种歧义并提问，而不是采纳一个看似合理但非预期的解读。现有金融基准无法衡量这一点，因为每个问题只有一个金标准答案，无法将解决歧义的智能体与猜测常见解读的智能体区分开，我们将这一盲点称为单一金标准幻觉。我们发布 FinInteract，一个包含 173 个实例的双语（英语/中文）基准；它在五类歧义分类体系下为每个问题配对默认解读与预期解读，并评估智能体是否引出正确澄清并随后将其整合。将相同输出按照默认解读而非预期解读重新评分，会使 GPT-4o 的准确率膨胀 3.1 倍，从而证实该幻觉。除此之外，我们发现：一旦提供解读，模型回答准确率超过 90%，但当它们必须自行引出解读时最高仅为 28.9%；模型在分类体系上的定位不均衡，该分类体系在实体范围和指标定义方面具有足够统计功效，而在其他方面仅为探索性；并且，以歧义类别为条件可在推理时和训练时提升消解效果。

Original abstract: Large language model agents increasingly answer financial questions by searching regulatory filings. Such questions are often deceptively under-specified: Meta Platforms' "operating income" is $46.75B consolidated but $62.87B for the Family of Apps segment, and each reading is exactly verifiable against the filing. A capable agent should recognize the ambiguity and ask, rather than commit to a plausible but unintended reading. Existing financial benchmarks cannot measure this, because one gold answer per question cannot separate agents that resolve the ambiguity from those that guess the common reading, a blind spot we call the single-gold illusion. We release FinInteract, a bilingual (English/Chinese) benchmark of 173 instances that pairs each question with a default and an intended interpretation across a five-category ambiguity taxonomy, and grades whether an agent elicits the right clarification and then integrates it. Re-grading identical outputs against the default rather than the intended reading inflates GPT-4o's accuracy by 3.1 times, confirming the illusion. Beyond it, we find that models answer above 90% once the interpretation is supplied but at most 28.9% when they must elicit it themselves, that targeting is uneven across a taxonomy well powered for entity scope and metric definition and exploratory elsewhere, and that conditioning on the ambiguity category improves resolution at both inference and training time.

</details>


### [50] [Synthesizing Reactive Character Behaviors for Continuous Games via Programmatic Policy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24025)
*Maxim Gumin,Hsueh-Ti Derek Liu,Victor Zordan,Daniel Ritchie*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: To be presented at SIGGRAPH Asia 2026

DOI: [10.1145/3829340.3842379](https://doi.org/10.1145/3829340.3842379)

Selection reason: 用coding agent结合程序枚举搜索生成可读策略程序，涉及代码合成agent与程序搜索。

TL;DR: 提出智能体草图法，在领域特定语言上结合枚举与编码智能体搜索连续游戏策略程序，在14个连续游戏上优于单独枚举或单独智能体，生成可编辑且有效的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏AI依赖手工编写行为树/状态机/脚本，而强化学习产生难以训练和编辑的不透明神经控制器；需要兼具可读、可编辑与自动搜索能力的策略表示。

Method: 设计面向连续空间游戏策略的领域特定语言，围绕反应式几何决策与方向最大化等高阶构造；引入合成反模式去除冗余程序形式；结合自底向上符号枚举与编码智能体自顶向下引导，形成智能体草图：智能体提出高层结构，枚举器填充局部槽位。

Result: 在14个连续游戏（经典控制到多智能体足球）基准上，纯枚举通常比单独编码智能体更高效，而组合方法显著优于两者。

Conclusion: 程序化策略搜索可作为游戏AI的实用创作工具：设计者只需指定奖励函数，系统即可发现有效、可移植、可编辑且常出人意料的行为。

中文摘要：我们提出了一种方法，用于将连续游戏中的反应式角色行为合成为紧凑、人类可读的程序。游戏AI实践仍然严重依赖手工编写的行为树、状态机和脚本，而学术强化学习通常产生不透明的神经控制器，这些控制器训练成本高且难以编辑。我们的方法通过直接在一个用于连续空间游戏策略的领域特定语言上进行搜索来弥合这一差距。该语言围绕反应式几何决策设计，并包含诸如方向最大化之类的高阶构造。这些构造有助于将连续行为空间离散化为可枚举的程序结构。为使程序搜索可行，我们引入大量综合反模式，这些反模式在保留行为覆盖的同时去除冗余程序形式。我们进一步将自底向上的符号枚举与来自编码智能体的自顶向下引导相结合。我们由此得到的方法——智能体草图（agentic sketching）——让智能体提出高层策略结构，并调用枚举器来完成局部程序槽位。我们在包含14个连续游戏的基准上评估该方法，范围从经典控制任务到多智能体足球。我们发现，纯枚举通常比单独使用编码智能体更高效，而组合方法则显著优于两者。我们的结果表明，程序化策略搜索可以成为游戏AI的实用创作工具：设计者指定奖励函数，系统发现可编辑的行为，这些行为有效、可移植，且常常出人意料。

Original abstract: We present a method for synthesizing reactive character behaviors for continuous games as compact, human-readable programs. Game AI practice still relies heavily on manually authored behavior trees, state machines, and scripts, while academic reinforcement learning typically produces opaque neural controllers that are expensive to train and difficult to edit. Our approach bridges this gap by searching directly over a domain-specific language for continuous-space game policies. The language is designed around reactive geometric decisions and includes higher-order constructs such as direction maximization. These constructs help discretize a continuous behavior space into enumerable program structures. To make program search practical, we introduce a large set of synthesis antipatterns that remove redundant program forms while preserving behavioral coverage. We further combine bottom-up symbolic enumeration with top-down guidance from a coding agent. Our resulting method, agentic sketching, has the agent propose high-level policy structure and call an enumerator to complete local program slots. We evaluate the method on a benchmark of 14 continuous games, ranging from classic control tasks to multi-agent football. We find that pure enumeration is often more efficient than using a coding agent alone, while the combined method substantially outperforms both. Our results suggest that programmatic policy search can be a practical authoring tool for game AI: designers specify reward functions, and the system discovers editable behaviors that are effective, portable, and often surprising.

</details>


### [51] [Structured Decomposition for Reliable LLM-Generated Access Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24036)
*Vatsal Gupta,Darshan Sreenivasamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM将自然语言策略分解并生成/验证Rego代码，包含结构化分解、测试生成与可靠性验证。

TL;DR: 该文提出基于LLM的NLACP到OPA Rego翻译系统，通过结构化分解与模式感知验证，在严格可部署正确性下将端到端策略正确率从15.3%提升至50.3%，并显著改善deny、ownership、status等安全关键模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合人类可读访问需求与机器可执行策略即代码（PaC）之间的差距，提升LLM生成授权策略的部署可靠性与安全正确性。

Method: 构建模块化端到端流水线，涵盖策略检测、组件提取、模式验证、linting、编译、自动化测试生成与执行；在372条ACRE完整访问控制语句上与直接单提示LLM基线对比，以分离结构化分解和模式感知验证的贡献。

Result: 端到端策略正确率50.3%（基线15.3%，3.3倍提升）；deny语义正确率87.5%（基线37.5%）；ownership条件100%（基线40%）；status限定条件100%（基线55.6%）。正确性需同时满足编译、linting及正向/负向测试。

Conclusion: 结构化分解与模式感知验证对提高LLM生成的授权策略可靠性具有关键作用。

中文摘要：本文提出了一种基于LLM的系统，可将自然语言访问控制策略（NLACP）转换为用于Open Policy Agent（OPA）的可执行Rego代码。它提供了一个模块化的端到端流水线，用于策略检测、组件提取、模式验证、linting、编译以及自动化测试生成与执行。该系统旨在弥合人类可读的访问需求与机器可执行的策略即代码（PaC）之间的差距，重点关注部署可靠性与安全正确性。我们在372条ACRE完整的访问控制语句上评估该系统，这些语句具有非空的主体、动作和资源标注，并将其与直接单提示LLM基线进行比较，以分离结构化分解和模式感知验证的贡献。该系统实现了50.3%的端到端策略正确率，而基线为15.3%，相当于3.3倍的提升。仅当策略满足编译、linting以及正向和负向测试时，才会被计为正确，这使其成为可部署正确性的严格度量。在安全关键模式上，系统为87.5%的deny策略生成正确的deny语义（基线：37.5%），为100%的ownership限定策略生成ownership条件（基线：40%），并为100%的status限定策略生成status限定条件（基线：55.6%）。这些结果表明，结构化分解和模式感知验证在提高LLM生成的授权策略的可靠性方面起着关键作用。

Original abstract: This paper presents an LLM-based system that translates natural-language access control policies (NLACPs) into executable Rego code for Open Policy Agent (OPA). It provides a modular, end-to-end pipeline for policy detection, component extraction, schema validation, linting, compilation, and automated test generation and execution. The system is designed to bridge the gap between human-readable access requirements and machine-enforceable policy-as-code (PaC), with a focus on deployment reliability and security correctness.
  We evaluate the system on 372 ACRE-complete access control statements with non-null subject, action, and resource annotations against a direct single-prompt LLM baseline to isolate the contribution of structured decomposition and schema-aware validation. The system achieves a 50.3% end-to-end policy correctness rate, compared with 15.3% for the baseline, representing a 3.3x improvement. A policy is counted as correct only if it satisfies compilation, linting, and both positive and negative tests, making this a strict measure of deployable correctness.
  On security-critical patterns, the system generates correct deny semantics for 87.5% of deny policies (baseline: 37.5%), ownership conditions for 100% of ownership-qualified policies (baseline: 40%), and status-qualified conditions for 100% of status-qualified policies (baseline: 55.6%). These results indicate that structured decomposition and schema-aware validation play a critical role in improving the reliability of LLM-generated authorization policies.

</details>


### [52] [Incremental Consistency Execution for Autonomous Intelligent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24090)
*Cheng Li,Jiexiong Liu,Yixuan Chen,Ziheng Huang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向LLM/工具长程执行的增量一致性复用与依赖掩码方法，可迁移到agent workflow优化与去冗余。

TL;DR: 提出基于任务事实契约、字段级依赖掩码与状态扰动不变域的增量一致性执行方法，减少昂贵组件调用与端到端延迟，同时保持高一致性并降低错误重用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程自主智能系统依赖异构组件且外部状态持续变化，全量重执行会带来大量冗余计算，因此需要安全、细粒度的增量执行机制。

Method: 初始验证执行后，为关键输入构建保守不变域以判断下游结果能否安全更新；必要时仅重算最小受影响输出字段，并用等价屏障阻止不必要传播；提交时版本一致性门保障副作用操作安全。

Result: 在工业故障诊断、企业分析和基于大语言模型的多工具助手场景中，显著减少昂贵组件调用与端到端延迟，同时保持高一致性和低错误重用率。

Conclusion: 该方法为动态外部状态下的异构长时程系统提供了安全高效的增量一致性执行机制，在降低计算开销的同时兼顾结果一致性与副作用安全。

中文摘要：长时程自主智能系统依赖大语言模型、数据库、外部API和规则引擎等异构组件，而其外部状态在执行过程中持续变化。每次变化后重新执行整个工作流会引入大量冗余计算。本文提出一种基于任务事实契约、字段级依赖掩码和状态扰动结果不变域的增量一致性执行方法。在初始验证执行后，系统为关键输入构造保守不变域，并利用这些不变域判断下游结果是否可以在不重新调用昂贵组件的情况下安全更新。当需要重新执行时，仅重新计算最小受影响输出字段，并设置等价屏障以防止不必要的下游传播。提交时版本一致性门进一步确保具有副作用的操作的安全性。在工业故障诊断、企业分析和基于大语言模型的多工具助手上的实验表明，所提方法显著减少昂贵组件调用和端到端延迟，同时保持高一致性和低错误重用率。

Original abstract: Long-horizon autonomous intelligent systems rely on heterogeneous components such as large language models, databases, external APIs, and rule engines, while their external states continuously change during execution. Re-executing the entire workflow after every change introduces substantial redundant computation. This paper proposes an incremental consistency execution method based on task fact contracts, field-level dependency masks, and state perturbation result invariant domains. After an initial verified execution, the system constructs conservative invariant domains for critical inputs and uses them to determine whether downstream results can be safely renewed without re-invoking expensive components. When re-execution is required, only the smallest affected output fields are recomputed, and an equivalence barrier prevents unnecessary downstream propagation. A submission-time version consistency gate further ensures the safety of side-effecting actions. Experiments on industrial fault diagnosis, enterprise analytics, and LLM-based multi-tool assistants show that the proposed method significantly reduces expensive component calls and end-to-end latency while maintaining high consistency and low incorrect-reuse rates.

</details>


### [53] [DocMIDE: Learning Multi-Hop Implicit Derivation in Visually Rich Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24092)
*Jeremy Cerwin Wang,Wai Kit Wong,Jeff Kai Tai Tang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 训练VLM按plan-retrieve-derive执行并奖励中间步骤，对agent规划/检索/验证的多步推理有启发。

TL;DR: DocMIDE通过“计划-检索-推导”结构和基于规则的四组件奖励优化紧凑视觉语言模型，在隐式文档抽取中显著提升多跳视觉推理准确率，并验证奖励中间步骤的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实文档处理依赖预定义模式，但关键目标字段常无直接视觉对应，需要多跳推导；现有方法在标准微调后仍无法正确检索视觉证据，或正确检索后跳过中间推导步骤。

Method: 提出DocMIDE微调框架，约束生成遵循计划-检索-推导结构，并采用Group Relative Policy Optimization，在四组件规则奖励下，依据验证过的参考轨迹对输出格式、检索证据块、每个中间推导步骤和最终值进行评分。

Result: 在4,151对隐式抽取基准上，DocMIDE仅用少量标注示例使Qwen3.5-4B准确率从70.8%升至95.9%，并可迁移到第二种骨干架构；仅监督示范在所有测试预算下均无法弥合差距。

Conclusion: 奖励中间推导步骤，而非仅提供监督示范，是解决多跳视觉隐式抽取的关键。

中文摘要：现实世界的文档处理系统依赖于僵化、预定义的schema，但关键目标字段往往在页面上没有直接的视觉对应物。抽取这些隐式值需要多跳推导，例如聚合子类别或对视觉标记进行推理。现有方法能够处理显式文本片段或简单隐式查询，但即使经过标准微调，它们在多跳视觉推理上仍然失败：模型检索到错误的视觉证据，或者正确检索到证据后却跳过了推导的中间步骤。为解决这一问题，我们提出DocMIDE，这是一个微调框架，训练紧凑的视觉语言模型在推导答案之前显式地检索视觉证据。DocMIDE将生成约束为“计划-检索-推导”结构，并在一个四组件、基于规则的奖励下用Group Relative Policy Optimization对其进行优化，该奖励根据经过验证的参考轨迹对输出格式、检索到的证据块、每一个中间推导步骤以及最终值进行评分。在一个包含4,151对样本的隐式抽取基准上，DocMIDE仅用少量标注示例就将Qwen3.5-4B的准确率从70.8%提升到95.9%，并迁移到第二种骨干架构。在我们测试的所有预算下，仅靠监督示范都无法弥合这一差距；奖励中间步骤才是关键。

Original abstract: Real-world document processing systems rely on rigid, predefined schemas, yet critical target fields often lack direct visual counterparts on the page. Extracting these implicit values requires multi-hop derivation, such as aggregating sub-categories or reasoning over visual marks. While existing methods handle explicit text spans or simple implicit queries, they fail at multi-hop visual reasoning even after standard fine-tuning: models retrieve incorrect visual evidence, or retrieve it correctly and then skip the intermediate steps of the derivation. To address this, we introduce DocMIDE, a fine-tuning framework that trains compact vision-language models to retrieve visual evidence explicitly before deriving an answer. DocMIDE constrains generation to a plan-retrieve-derive structure and optimizes it with Group Relative Policy Optimization under a four-component, rule-based reward that scores output format, the retrieved evidence block, every intermediate derivation step, and the final value against a verified reference trace. On a 4,151-pair implicit extraction benchmark, DocMIDE raises accuracy from 70.8% to 95.9% on Qwen3.5-4B from only a small set of annotated examples, and transfers to a second backbone architecture. Supervised demonstrations alone do not close this gap at any budget we tested; rewarding the intermediate steps is what does.

</details>


### [54] [When More Evidence Hurts: Publication-Bias Drift and Principled Stopping for Biomedical Causal Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24101)
*Fred Sun,Shangqi Guo*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出带漂移感知与两层停止策略的检索agent，涉及证据检索深度、PRM和停止控制。

TL;DR: 论文形式化并验证了检索越深越易因发表偏倚产生“证据漂移”的假阳性上升现象，并提出DACG-agent通过因果图与两层停止策略降低漂移、提升准确率并减少检索步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生物医学证据综合依赖文献检索，但文献对阳性结果的系统性偏倚会使更深检索在零效应问题上反而更容易产生假阳性，因此需要漂移感知的检索控制。

Method: 形式化“证据漂移”并证明其在标准发表偏倚模型下随检索深度严格递增；DACG-agent从PubMed摘要增量构建因果知识图谱，并采用两层停止策略：KL散度后验收敛监控与Bradley--Terry过程奖励模型在线下降检测。

Result: 在140个Cochrane查询上，漂移随检索预算从3步增至20步由7.9%升至15.7%；DACG-agent将漂移降至6.4%，零效应准确率提高21个百分点（40.0%→61.4%），检索步骤减少67%，总体准确率由61.4%升至69.3%（95% CI 61--77），仿真验证漂移结果可迁移至noisy-OR聚合器。

Conclusion: 检索深度需受证据质量约束；漂移感知的因果图智能体与互补两层停止策略可缓解发表偏倚导致的假阳性，并同时提升准确率与检索效率。
